```
1	import { scryptSync, randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'
2	
3	// scrypt with per-user salt — matches the existing farmer login pattern
4	// Format on disk: "salt_hex:hash_hex"
5	export function hashPassword(password: string): string {
6	  const salt = randomBytes(16).toString('hex')
7	  const hash = scryptSync(password, salt, 64).toString('hex')
8	  return `${salt}:${hash}`
9	}
10	
11	export function verifyPassword(password: string, stored: string): boolean {
12	  try {
13	    const [salt, hash] = stored.split(':')
14	    if (!salt || !hash) return false
15	    const hashBuffer = Buffer.from(hash, 'hex')
16	    const derived = scryptSync(password, salt, 64)
17	    if (hashBuffer.length !== derived.length) return false
18	    return timingSafeEqual(hashBuffer, derived)
19	  } catch {
20	    return false
21	  }
22	}
23	
```

```
1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { hashPassword } from '@/lib/password'
4	import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
5	import { setSessionCookie } from '@/lib/session'
6	import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'
7	
8	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
9	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
10	
11	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
12	  // Lightweight anti-abuse: 10 registrations per IP per hour
13	  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'
14	  if (!rateLimit(`reg:${ip}`, 10, 60 * 60 * 1000)) {
15	    return NextResponse.json(
16	      { error: 'Too many sign-up attempts. Try again in an hour.' },
17	      { status: 429 },
18	    )
19	  }
20	
21	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
22	  if (!body || typeof body !== 'object') {
23	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid request.' }, { status: 400 })
24	  }
25	
26	  const name = String(body.name ?? '').trim().slice(0, 80)
27	  const phone = normalizePhone(body.phone)
28	  const password = String(body.password ?? '')
29	
30	  if (!name) {
31	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please enter your name.' }, { status: 400 })
32	  }
33	  if (!phone) {
34	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })
35	  }
36	  if (password.length < 6) {
37	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password must be at least 6 characters.' }, { status: 400 })
38	  }
39	  if (password.length > 128) {
40	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password is too long.' }, { status: 400 })
41	  }
42	
43	  const supabase = createClient(
44	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
45	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
46	  )
47	
48	  // Reject duplicate phone (UNIQUE constraint also enforces, but check first
49	  // to give a clean error message)
50	  const { data: existing, error: lookupErr } = await supabase
51	    .from('consumers_auth')
52	    .select('id')
53	    .eq('phone', phone)
54	    .maybeSingle()
55	
56	  if (lookupErr) {
57	    console.error('[YFF register] lookup failed:', lookupErr.code, lookupErr.message)
58	    if (
59	      lookupErr.message?.includes('does not exist')
60	      || lookupErr.code === '42P01'
61	    ) {
62	      return NextResponse.json(
63	        { error: 'consumers_auth table is missing. Run scripts/consumer-auth-migration.sql in Supabase first.' },
64	        { status: 500 },
65	      )
66	    }
67	    return NextResponse.json(
68	      { error: `Database error: ${lookupErr.message}` },
69	      { status: 500 },
70	    )
71	  }
72	
73	  if (existing) {
74	    return NextResponse.json(
75	      { error: 'An account already exists for this phone. Please log in instead.' },
76	      { status: 409 },
77	    )
78	  }
79	
80	  const password_hash = hashPassword(password)
81	  const { data: created, error: insertErr } = await supabase
82	    .from('consumers_auth')
83	    .insert({ name, phone, password_hash })
84	    .select('id, name, phone')
85	    .single()
86	
87	  if (insertErr || !created) {
88	    console.error('[YFF register] insert failed:', insertErr?.code, insertErr?.message)
89	    // Surface the underlying error so we can debug instead of guessing
90	    return NextResponse.json(
91	      { error: insertErr?.message || 'Could not create account. Please try again.' },
92	      { status: 500 },
93	    )
94	  }
95	
96	  const res = NextResponse.json({
97	    ok: true,
98	    consumer: { id: created.id, name: created.name, phone: created.phone },
99	  })
100	  try {
101	    setSessionCookie(res, created.id)
102	  } catch (e) {
103	    const msg = e instanceof Error ? e.message : 'Session setup failed.'
104	    console.error('[YFF register] setSessionCookie failed:', msg)
105	    return NextResponse.json({ error: msg }, { status: 500 })
106	  }
107	  return res
108	}
109
```

```
## Session Summary: 2026-06-16 Interrupted Work

Based on the git history and uncommitted changes, I found that **work on a major courier/order-completion feature was interrupted on 2026-06-16 at ~18:33 UTC+5:30** and has left **7 modified files** uncommitted in your working directory.

### Task in Progress
**Goal:** Implement full-order lifecycle improvements, specifically the **"Farmer Couriers" delivery mode** (#11) and **order completion flows** for all delivery types.

The feature adds three distinct order completion patterns:
1. **Self-pickup**: Farmer taps "Picked Up" when buyer collects
2. **Courier**: Farmer ships the parcel; buyer confirms "Received" on order page  
3. **Home delivery**: Existing rider model (already working)

### Files Modified (Uncommitted)

| File | Status | What Changed |
|------|--------|--------------|
| `/src/app/api/consumer/orders/[id]/route.ts` | M | Added `shipped_at`, `received_at` fields to order query |
| `/src/app/api/consumer/orders/[id]/received/route.ts` | NEW | New endpoint: buyer confirms courier delivery receipt, sets `received_at` |
| `/src/app/api/farmer/orders/[id]/picked-up/route.ts` | NEW | New endpoint: farmer taps "Picked Up" for self-pickup, sets `collected_at` |
| `/src/app/api/farmer/orders/[id]/ship/route.ts` | NEW | New endpoint: farmer marks courier order shipped, sets `shipped_at` |
| `/src/app/consumer/orders/[id]/page.tsx` | M | Added `confirmingReceipt` state; calls `/api/consumer/orders/[id]/received`; added light polling (25s) to auto-refresh active orders |
| `/src/app/farmer/dashboard/page.tsx` | M | Refactored to show full listing rows inline; added `isResolved()` helper (order is done when collected/delivered/received); added `handleToggleListingSuspend()` and `handleMarkPickedUp()` handlers; changed query to fetch both pending AND approved orders, filtering out resolved ones |
| `/src/components/consumer/Cart.tsx` | M | Added "Farmer couriers it" (📦) button; both `home_delivery` and `courier` require address; updated checkout summary label logic |
| `/src/app/api/orders/place/route.ts` | M | Added courier delivery type validation; both home_delivery and courier need address |
| `/src/lib/translations.ts` | M | Added EN/TE strings: `confirmPickupDate`, `confirmDeliveryDate`, `chooseDateToApprove`, `awaitingPickup`, `awaitingDelivery`, `editDate` |
| `package.json` | M | Added Node flag: `REDACTED next dev` |
```

```
1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getFarmerSessionFromRequest } from '@/lib/farmer-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	// Farmer marks a courier order as shipped — used when they hand the parcel to a
11	// courier or post it themselves. Stamps shipped_at. The order STAYS in Active
12	// Orders (it is not yet resolved); it only leaves once the buyer confirms
13	// receipt via the consumer "Received" route, which stamps received_at.
14	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
15	  const session = getFarmerSessionFromRequest(req)
16	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
17	
18	  const { id } = await ctx.params
19	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
20	
21	  const supabase = createClient(
22	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
23	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
24	  )
25	
26	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
27	    .from('orders')
28	    .select('id, farmer_id, status, delivery_type, shipped_at')
29	    .eq('id', id)
30	    .maybeSingle()
31	
32	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
33	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
34	  if (order.farmer_id !== session.farmerId) {
35	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
36	  }
37	  if (order.delivery_type !== 'courier') {
38	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only a courier order can be marked shipped.' }, { status: 409 })
39	  }
40	  if (order.status !== 'approved') {
41	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Confirm the order before marking it shipped.' }, { status: 409 })
42	  }
43	  if (order.shipped_at) {
44	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order is already marked shipped.' }, { status: 409 })
45	  }
46	
47	  const { data: updated, error: updErr } = await supabase
48	    .from('orders')
49	    .update({ shipped_at: new Date().toISOString() })
50	    .eq('id', id)
51	    .eq('farmer_id', session.farmerId)
52	    .eq('status', 'approved')
53	    .is('shipped_at', null)
54	    .select('id, shipped_at')
55	
56	  if (updErr) {
57	    console.error('[YFF farmer/ship] update failed:', updErr.message)
58	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not mark shipped. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
59	  }
60	  if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {
61	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not mark shipped. Refresh and try again.' }, { status: 409 })
62	  }
63	
64	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, shipped_at: updated[0].shipped_at })
65	}
66
```

```
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	// Farmer marks a self-pickup order as collected with a single tap — used when
11	// the buyer comes to the farm and takes the produce. Unlike the OTP-based
12	// confirm-pickup route, this requires no code from the buyer; the farmer is
13	// trusted to mark their own pickup. Stamps collected_at, which moves the order
14	// from Active Orders to Order History.
15	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
16	  const session = getFarmerSessionFromRequest(req)
17	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
18	
19	  const { id } = await ctx.params
20	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
21	
22	  const supabase = createClient(
23	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
24	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
25	  )
26	
27	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
28	    .from('orders')
29	    .select('id, farmer_id, status, delivery_type, collected_at')
30	    .eq('id', id)
31	    .maybeSingle()
32	
33	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
34	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
35	  if (order.farmer_id !== session.farmerId) {
36	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
37	  }
38	  // Only self-pickup orders are completed this way. Rider deliveries are closed
39	  // at the door; courier orders are closed by the buyer tapping "Received".
40	  if (order.delivery_type !== 'self_pickup') {
41	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only a self-pickup order can be marked picked up here.' }, { status: 409 })
42	  }
43	  if (order.status !== 'approved') {
44	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only an approved order can be marked picked up.' }, { status: 409 })
45	  }
46	  if (order.collected_at) {
47	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order is already marked picked up.' }, { status: 409 })
48	  }
49	
50	  const { data: updated, error: updErr } = await supabase
51	    .from('orders')
52	    .update({ collected_at: new Date().toISOString() })
53	    .eq('id', id)
54	    .eq('farmer_id', session.farmerId)
55	    .eq('status', 'approved')
56	    .is('collected_at', null)
57	    .select('id, collected_at')
58	
59	  if (updErr) {
60	    console.error('[YFF farmer/picked-up] update failed:', updErr.message)
61	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not mark picked up. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
62	  }
63	  if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {
64	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not mark picked up. Refresh and try again.' }, { status: 409 })
65	  }
66	
67	  return NextResponse.json({ ok: true, collected_at: updated[0].collected_at })
68	}
```

```
7	
8	const UUID_RE = /^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i
9	
10	// Buyer confirms they received a shipped courier order. Only allowed once the
11	// farmer has marked it shipped (shipped_at set). Stamps received_at, which
12	// resolves the order and moves it to history on both sides.
13	export async function POST(req: NextRequest, ctx: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
14	  const session = getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req)
15	  if (!session) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Please log in.' }, { status: 401 })
16	
17	  const { id } = await ctx.params
18	  if (!id || !UUID_RE.test(id)) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid order id.' }, { status: 400 })
19	
20	  const supabase = createClient(
21	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
22	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
23	  )
24	
25	  const { data: order, error: loadErr } = await supabase
26	    .from('orders')
27	    .select('id, consumer_id, status, delivery_type, shipped_at, received_at')
28	    .eq('id', id)
29	    .maybeSingle()
30	
31	  if (loadErr) return NextResponse.json({ error: loadErr.message }, { status: 500 })
32	  if (!order) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Order not found.' }, { status: 404 })
33	  if (order.consumer_id !== session.consumerId) {
34	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Not your order.' }, { status: 403 })
35	  }
36	  if (order.delivery_type !== 'courier') {
37	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only a courier order is confirmed this way.' }, { status: 409 })
38	  }
39	  if (!order.shipped_at) {
40	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order has not been shipped yet.' }, { status: 409 })
41	  }
42	  if (order.received_at) {
43	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This order is already marked received.' }, { status: 409 })
44	  }
45	
46	  const { data: updated, error: updErr } = await supabase
47	    .from('orders')
48	    .update({ received_at: new Date().toISOString() })
49	    .eq('id', id)
50	    .eq('consumer_id', session.consumerId)
51	    .not('shipped_at', 'is', null)
52	    .is('received_at', null)
53	    .select('id, received_at')
54	
55	  if (updErr) {
56	    console.error('[YFF consumer/received] update failed:', updErr.message)
57	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not confirm receipt. Please try again.' }, { status: 500 })
58	  }
59	  if (!updated || updated.length === 0) {
60	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not confirm receipt. Refresh and try again.' }, { status: 409 })
61	  }
62
```

```
104	  decline_reason: string | null
105	  created_at: string
106	  delivery_type?: 'self_pickup' | 'home_delivery' | 'courier' | null
107	  delivery_status?: DeliveryStatus | null
108	  delivery_boy_id?: string | null
109	  fulfillment_date?: string | null
110	  collected_at?: string | null
111	  shipped_at?: string | null
112	  received_at?: string | null
113	}
114	
115	// An approved order is "resolved" (and so leaves the farmer's active list) once:
116	//   self_pickup   → the buyer collected it (collected_at set)
117	//   courier       → the buyer confirmed receipt (received_at set); note a
118	//                   shipped-but-unreceived courier order stays active
119	//   home_delivery → the rider delivered it (delivery_status 'delivered')
120	function isResolved(o: Order): boolean {
121	  if (o.status !== 'approved') return false
122	  if (o.delivery_type === 'home_delivery') return o.delivery_status === 'delivered'
123	  if (o.delivery_type === 'courier') return !!o.received_at
124	  return !!o.collected_at
125	}
126	
127	const UNIT_OPTIONS = [
128	  { value: 'kg', label: 'kg' },
129	  { value: 'gram', label: 'gram' },
130	  { value: 'piece', label: 'piece / నగ' },
131	  { value: 'bunch', label: 'bunch / కట్ట' },
132	  { value: 'litre', label: 'litre' },
133	]
134	
135	type PreviewData = {
136	  name: string
137	  variety: string
138	  emoji: string
139	  price: string
140	  method: string
141	  stock: string
142	}
143	
144	// 📦 is a generic "Other / ఇతర" icon so a farmer can list any produce
145	// even when no specific icon exists. Keep it last in the picker.
146	const EMOJI_OPTIONS = ['🍅', '🍌', '🥭', '🫑', '🥬', '🍆', '🥕', '🌽', '🧅', '🧄', '🥦', '🌿', '🍓', '🫒', '🌾', '🥥', '📦']
147	
148	async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
149	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
150	    const img = new Image()
151	    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
152	    img.onload = () => {
153	      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
154	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
155	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
156	      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
157	      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
158	      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
159	      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
160	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
161	      canvas.toBlob(
162	        (blob) => {
163	          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }
164	          const name = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')
165	          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))
166	        },
167	        'image/jpeg',
168	        quality,
169	      )
170	    }
171	    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }
172	    img.src = blobUrl
173	  })
174	}
175	
176	const isProfileComplete = (f: Farmer | null) =>
177	  !!f && f.name?.trim().length > 0 && f.village?.trim().length > 0
178	
179	export default function FarmerDashboard() {
180	  const router = useRouter()
181	  const { tx } = useLang()
182	  const [farmer, setFarmer] = useState<Farmer | null>(null)
183	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
184	  const [notFound, setNotFound] = useState(false)
185	  const [listings, setListings] = useState<DashboardListing[]>([])
186	  const [pendingOrders, setPendingOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
187	  const [approvedCount, setApprovedCount] = useState(0)
188	  const [totalRevenue, setTotalRevenue] = useState(0)
189	  const [ordersFilter, setOrdersFilter] = useState<'today' | 'week' | 'month'>('week')
190	  const [processingOrderId, setProcessingOrderId] = useState<string | null>(null)
191	  const [processingPaidId, setProcessingPaidId] = useState<string | null>(null)
192	  const [decliningOrder, setDecliningOrder] = useState<Order | null>(null)
193	  const [declineResult, setDeclineResult] = useState<
194	    { buyerName: string | null; amount: number | null; refundInitiated: boolean } | null
195	  >(null)
196	  const [demandBars, setDemandBars] = useState<DemandBar[]>([])
197	  const [monthlyRevenue, setMonthlyRevenue] = useState(0)
198	  const [monthlyOrderCount, setMonthlyOrderCount] = useState(0)
199	  const [weeklyEarnings, setWeeklyEarnings] = useState<number[]>([0, 0, 0, 0])
200	  const [showForm, setShowForm] = useState(false)
201	  const [showProfileEdit, setShowProfileEdit] = useState(false)
202	  const [showListings, setShowListings] = useState(false)
203	
204	  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
205	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
206	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
207	
208	    const { data: farmerData } = await supabase
209	      .from('farmers')
210	      .select('*')
211	      .eq('id', farmerId)
212	      .maybeSingle()
213	
214	    if (!farmerData) { setNotFound(true); setLoading(false); return }
215	    setFarmer(farmerData)
216	
217	    const monthStart = new Date()
218	    monthStart.setDate(1)
219	    monthStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
220	
221	    const [listingsRes, pendingRes, approvedRes, intentsRes, monthlyRes] = await Promise.all([
222	      // Full (lightweight) listing rows so the dashboard can show them inline
223	      // with a quick suspend/resume; the active-listings count is derived below.
224	      supabase.from('produce_listings').select('id, name, emoji, status, price_tier_1_price, unit, stock_qty').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
225	      // Active orders = still pending, OR approved but not yet picked up/delivered.
226	      // Approved orders stay here so the farmer keeps the scheduled date in view
227	      // until the buyer collects (or the rider delivers).
228	      supabase.from('orders').select('*').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).in('status', ['pending', 'approved']).order('created_at', { ascending: false }),
229	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', new Date(Date.now() - 7 * 86400000).toISOString()),
230	      supabase.from('demand_intents').select('crop_name, quantity_kg').eq('region_slug', farmerData.region_slug).eq('fulfilled', false),
231	      supabase.from('orders').select('id, total_price, created_at').eq('farmer_id', farmerData.id).eq('status', 'approved').gte('created_at', monthStart.toISOString()),
232	    ])
233	
234	    setListings((listingsRes.data ?? []) as DashboardListing[])
235	    // Drop approved orders that are already resolved (collected / delivered).
236	    const activeOrders = (pendingRes.data ?? []).filter((o) => !isResolved(o as Order)) as Order[]
237	    setPendingOrders(activeOrders)
238	    const approved = approvedRes.data ?? []
239	    setApprovedCount(approved.length)
240	    setTotalRevenue(approved.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
241	
242	    // Monthly earnings
243	    const monthly = monthlyRes.data ?? []
244	    setMonthlyRevenue(monthly.reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0))
245	    setMonthlyOrderCount(monthly.length)
246	
247	    // Break into 4 weekly buckets (days 1-7, 8-14, 15-21, 22+)
248	    const weeks = [0, 0, 0, 0]
249	    for (const o of monthly) {
250	      const day = new Date(o.created_at).getDate()
251	      const bucket = day <= 7 ? 0 : day <= 14 ? 1 : day <= 21 ? 2 : 3
252	      weeks[bucket] += o.total_price ?? 0
253	    }
254	    setWeeklyEarnings(weeks)
255	
256	    const map: Record<string, number> = {}
257	    for (const row of intentsRes.data ?? []) {
258	      map[row.crop_name] = (map[row.crop_name] ?? 0) + (Number(row.quantity_kg) || 0)
259	    }
260	    setDemandBars(
261	      Object.entries(map)
262	        .map(([crop_name, total_qty]) => ({ crop_name, total_qty }))
263	        .sort((a, b) => b.total_qty - a.total_qty)
264	        .slice(0, 5)
265	    )
266	
267	    setLoading(false)
268	  }, [router])
269	
270	  useEffect(() => { loadDashboard() }, [loadDashboard])
271	
272	  // Auto-open the profile edit modal the first time an incomplete farmer lands here.
273	  useEffect(() => {
274	    if (!loading && farmer && !isProfileComplete(farmer)) {
275	      setShowProfileEdit(true)
276	    }
277	  }, [loading, farmer])
278	
279	  // Realtime subscription: new orders + payment status changes.
280	  // Replaces the old "consumer opens WhatsApp to notify farmer" flow.
281	  // Fires a browser notification when:
282	  //   - A new pending order is inserted
283	  //   - An existing order's payment_status flips to payment_claimed (incl. retries)
284	  useEffect(() => {
285	    if (!farmer) return
286	
287	    const fireNotification = (title: string, body: string) => {
288	      if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
289	      if (!('Notification' in window)) return
290	      if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') return
291	      try {
292	        new Notification(title, { body, icon: '/icon-192.png', tag: 'yff-order' })
293	      } catch { /* some browsers throw on background tabs — ignore */ }
294	    }
295	
296	    const channel = supabase
297	      .channel(`orders_${farmer.id}`)
298	      .on(
299	        'postgres_changes',
300	        { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'orders', filter: `farmer_id=eq.${farmer.id}` },
301	        (payload) => {
302	          const row = payload.new as Order
303	          if (row.status !== 'pending') return
304	          setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.some((o) => o.id === row.id) ? prev : [row, ...prev])
305	          fireNotification(
306	            `New order from ${row.buyer_name ?? 'buyer'}`,
307	            `${row.produce_name ?? ''} ${row.quantity ?? ''} ${row.unit ?? ''}${row.total_price ? ` · ₹${row.total_price}` : ''}`.trim(),
308	          )
309	        },
310	      )
311	      .on(
312	        'postgres_changes',
313	        { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'orders', filter: `farmer_id=eq.${farmer.id}` },
314	        (payload) => {
315	          const row = payload.new as Order
316	          const prev = payload.old as Partial<Order>
317	          // Declined/cancelled, or an approved order that's now picked up /
318	          // delivered — it's resolved, so drop it from the active list.
319	          if ((row.status !== 'pending' && row.status !== 'approved') || isResolved(row)) {
320	            setPendingOrders((cur) => cur.filter((o) => o.id !== row.id))
321	            return
322	          }
323	          // Still active (pending, or approved-and-awaiting): update or insert.
324	          setPendingOrders((cur) => {
325	            const exists = cur.some((o) => o.id === row.id)
326	            return exists ? cur.map((o) => o.id === row.id ? row : o) : [row, ...cur]
327	          })
328	          // Fire notification when buyer claims payment (covers initial pay AND retry)
329	          const becameClaimed =
330	            (row.payment_status === 'payment_claimed' || row.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation')
331	            && prev.payment_status !== row.payment_status
332	          if (becameClaimed) {
333	            fireNotification(
334	              `Buyer paid — verify payment`,
335	              `${row.buyer_name ?? 'Buyer'} sent ₹${row.total_price ?? '?'} for ${row.produce_name ?? 'order'}`,
336	            )
337	          }
338	        },
339	      )
340	      .subscribe()
341	
342	    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }
343	  }, [farmer])
344	
345	  // Safety net for the realtime subscription: on slow/spotty 4G the websocket
346	  // can silently drop while the farmer is on another app. Refetch whenever the
347	  // dashboard tab regains focus so they never miss a new order.
348	  useEffect(() => {
349	    const refetch = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') loadDashboard() }
350	    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refetch)
351	    window.addEventListener('focus', refetch)
352	    return () => {
353	      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refetch)
354	      window.removeEventListener('focus', refetch)
355	    }
356	  }, [loadDashboard])
357	
358	  const handleLogout = () => {
359	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_id')
360	    localStorage.removeItem('yff_farmer_slug')
361	    router.replace('/farmer/login')
362	  }
363	
364	  // Quick suspend/resume from the dashboard's inline produce list. Mirrors the
365	  // Manage Listings modal: flips 'suspended_by_farmer' ⇄ 'available'.
366	  const handleToggleListingSuspend = async (id: string, currentStatus: string) => {
367	    const next = currentStatus === 'suspended_by_farmer' ? 'available' : 'suspended_by_farmer'
368	    setListings((prev) => prev.map((l) => (l.id === id ? { ...l, status: next } : l)))
369	    const { error } = await supabase.from('produce_listings').update({ status: next }).eq('id', id)
370	    if (error) { void loadDashboard() } // re-sync on failure
371	  }
372	
373	  // Approving requires the farmer to first set a pickup/delivery date — that
374	  // date is saved alongside the approval and shown to the buyer. The order then
375	  // stays in the active list (now marked approved) until it's picked up or
376	  // delivered, so the farmer keeps the schedule in view.
377	  const handleApprove = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
378	    if (!date) return
379	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
380	    await supabase
381	      .from('orders')
382	      .update({ status: 'approved', fulfillment_date: date, confirmed_at: new Date().toISOString() })
383	      .eq('id', orderId)
384	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
385	      prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, status: 'approved', fulfillment_date: date } : o)),
386	    )
387	    setApprovedCount((c) => c + 1)
388	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
389	  }
390	
391	  // Self-pickup: farmer taps "Picked Up" when the buyer collects. Stamps
392	  // collected_at server-side, which resolves the order — drop it from the
393	  // active list (it now appears in Order History).
394	  const handleMarkPickedUp = async (orderId: string) => {
395	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
396	    const res = await fetch(`/api/farmer/orders/${orderId}/picked-up`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' })
397	    if (res.ok) {
398	      setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
399	    } else {
400	      void loadDashboard() // re-sync on failure
401	    }
402	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
403	  }
404	
405	  // Courier: farmer taps "Shipped" when they hand the parcel over. Stamps
406	  // shipped_at but the order stays active (awaiting the buyer's "Received").
407	  const handleMarkShipped = async (orderId: string) => {
408	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
409	    const res = await fetch(`/api/farmer/orders/${orderId}/ship`, { method: 'POST', credentials: 'same-origin' })
410	    if (res.ok) {
411	      const json = (await res.json().catch(() => ({}))) as { shipped_at?: string }
412	      const shippedAt = json.shipped_at ?? new Date().toISOString()
413	      setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, shipped_at: shippedAt } : o)))
414	    } else {
415	      void loadDashboard() // re-sync on failure
416	    }
417	    setProcessingOrderId(null)
418	  }
419	
420	  // Farmer sets/clears the pickup-or-delivery date for an order. Saved
421	  // immediately so the consumer sees the exact date the farmer chose. An empty
422	  // string clears it back to null.
423	  const handleSetFulfillmentDate = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
424	    const value = date || null
425	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
426	      prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, fulfillment_date: value } : o)),
427	    )
428	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ fulfillment_date: value }).eq('id', orderId)
429	  }
430	
431	  const handleConfirmDecline = async (orderId: string, reason: string) => {
432	    const declined = decliningOrder
433	    setProcessingOrderId(orderId)
434	    // Declining runs server-side: it returns the reserved stock and, for a
435	    // paid order, issues a REAL Razorpay refund (the secret can't live in the
436	    // browser). If the refund fails the order stays pending so we can retry.
437	    try {
438	      const res = await fetch(`/api/farmer/orders/${orderId}/decline`, {
439	        method: 'POST',
440	        credentials: 'same-origin',
441	        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
442	        body: JSON.stringify({ reason }),
443	      })
444	      const json = await res.json().catch(() => ({}))
445	      if (!res.ok) {
446	        alert(json.error || 'Could not decline the order. Please try again. / ఆర్డర్ తిరస్కరించలేకపోయాం. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.')
447	        return
448	      }
449	      // Whether a refund went out (real Razorpay refund, or 'initiated' for
450	      // other paid methods) so we can confirm it to the farmer in plain terms.
451	      const refundInitiated = !!json.refunded || !!json.refundStatus
452	      setPendingOrders((prev) => prev.filter((o) => o.id !== orderId))
453	      setDeclineResult({
454	        buyerName: declined?.buyer_name ?? null,
455	        amount: declined?.total_price ?? null,
456	        refundInitiated,
457	      })
458	      setDecliningOrder(null)
459	    } catch {
460	      alert('Network error. Please try again. / నెట్‌వర్క్ సమస్య. మళ్ళీ ప్రయత్నించండి.')
461	    } finally {
462	      setProcessingOrderId(null)
463	    }
464	  }
465	
466	  const handleMarkPaid = async (orderId: string) => {
467	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
468	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ payment_status: 'completed', paid_at: new Date().toISOString() }).eq('id', orderId)
469	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
470	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: 'completed' } : o)
471	    )
472	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
473	  }
474	
475	  const handleUpdatePaymentStatus = async (orderId: string, status: 'completed' | 'failed' | 'pending') => {
476	    setProcessingPaidId(orderId)
477	    const update: Record<string, string> = { payment_status: status }
478	    if (status === 'completed') {
479	      update.status = 'approved'
480	      update.paid_at = new Date().toISOString()
481	      update.confirmed_at = new Date().toISOString()
482	    }
483	    await supabase.from('orders').update(update).eq('id', orderId)
484	    setPendingOrders((prev) =>
485	      prev.map((o) => o.id === orderId ? { ...o, payment_status: status, ...(status === 'completed' ? { status: 'approved' } : {}) } : o)
486	    )
487	    setProcessingPaidId(null)
488	  }
489	
490	  const filteredPendingOrders = pendingOrders.filter((o) => {
491	    const t = new Date(o.created_at).getTime()
492	    const now = Date.now()
493	    if (ordersFilter === 'today') {
494	      const todayStart = new Date()
495	      todayStart.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
496	      return t >= todayStart.getTime()
497	    }
498	    if (ordersFilter === 'week') return t >= now - 7 * 86400000
499	    return t >= now - 30 * 86400000
500	  })
501	
502	  if (loading) return <LoadingScreen />
503	  if (notFound) return <FarmerNotFound onLogout={handleLogout} />
504	
505	  // The list now also holds approved-but-unresolved orders; the stat card should
506	  // still reflect only the orders that genuinely need a response.
507	  const pendingCount = pendingOrders.filter((o) => o.status === 'pending').length
508	  // Active = visible-to-buyers listings, derived so the count stays in sync when
509	  // the farmer suspends/resumes from the inline produce list below.
510	  const activeListings = listings.filter((l) => l.status === 'available').length
511	
512	  const profileComplete = isProfileComplete(farmer)
513	  const displayName = farmer!.name?.trim() || tx.welcome
514	
515	  return (
516	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
517	      {/* Header */}
518	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
519	        <div className="flex justify-end mb-2">
520	          <LanguageToggle />
521	        </div>
522	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
523	          <div>
524	            <p className="text-green-400 text-xs font-semibold mb-0.5 uppercase tracking-wide">
525	              {tx.farmerDashboard}
526	            </p>
527	            <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">{displayName}</h1>
528	            <p className="text-green-300 text-sm mt-0.5">
529	              {profileComplete
530	                ? `${farmer!.village}, ${farmer!.district}`
531	                : tx.completeProfilePrompt}
532	            </p>
533	            <p className="text-green-500 text-xs mt-1">+91 {farmer!.phone}</p>
534	          </div>
535	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
536	            {profileComplete ? (
537	              <Link
538	                href={`/farmer/${farmer!.slug}`}
539	                className="bg-white text-green-800 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl"
540	              >
541	                {tx.viewProfile} ↗
542	              </Link>
543	            ) : (
544	              <span className="bg-amber-400 text-amber-900 text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded-full">
545	                {tx.incomplete}
546	              </span>
547	            )}
548	            <button
549	              onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
550	              className="text-white text-xs underline"
551	            >
552	              {tx.editProfile}
553	            </button>
554	            <Link href="/farmer/complaints" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 bg-amber-400 text-green-950 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full shadow-md active:bg-amber-500">
555	              🛟 {tx.complaints}
556	            </Link>
557	            <button onClick={handleLogout} className="text-green-500 text-xs underline">
558	              {tx.logout}
559	            </button>
560	          </div>
561	        </div>
562	      </div>
563	
564	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
565	        {/* Notification permission banner — shown only if browser supports it
566	            and the farmer hasn't decided yet. Permission must be requested via
567	            a user gesture so we can't auto-call it on mount. */}
568	        <NotificationPermissionBanner />
569	
570	        {/* Complete profile banner */}
571	        {!profileComplete && (
572	          <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-start gap-3">
573	            <span className="text-2xl flex-shrink-0">📝</span>
574	            <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
575	              <h3 className="font-extrabold text-amber-900 text-base leading-tight">
576	                {tx.completeProfileTitle}
577	              </h3>
578	              <p className="text-amber-700 text-xs mt-0.5">
579	                {tx.completeProfileHelp}
580	              </p>
581	              <button
582	                onClick={() => setShowProfileEdit(true)}
583	                className="mt-3 bg-amber-600 text-white font-bold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm"
584	              >
585	                {tx.fillDetails}
586	              </button>
587	            </div>
588	          </div>
589	        )}
590	
591	        {/* Stat cards */}
592	        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
593	          <button
594	            onClick={() => setShowListings(true)}
595	            className="border-green-200 bg-green-50 border rounded-2xl p-4 text-left active:bg-green-100 relative"
596	          >
597	            <div className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800">{activeListings}</div>
598	            <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1 leading-tight">{tx.activeListings}</div>
599	            <div className="text-[11px] font-bold text-green-700 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1">
600	              {tx.manage} <span aria-hidden>→</span>
601	            </div>
602	          </button>
603	          {[
604	            { label: tx.pendingOrders, value: pendingCount, color: pendingCount > 0 ? 'border-orange-300 bg-orange-50' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50', vcolor: pendingCount > 0 ? 'text-orange-700' : 'text-gray-500' },
605	            { label: tx.approvedThisWeek, value: approvedCount, color: 'border-green-200 bg-green-50', vcolor: 'text-green-800' },
606	            { label: tx.totalRevenue, value: totalRevenue > 0 ? `₹${totalRevenue}` : '—', color: 'border-purple-200 bg-purple-50', vcolor: 'text-purple-800' },
607	          ].map((s) => (
608	            <div key={s.label} className={`${s.color} border rounded-2xl p-4`}>
609	              <div className={`text-3xl font-black ${s.vcolor}`}>{s.value}</div>
610	              <div className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800 mt-1 leading-tight">{s.label}</div>
611	            </div>
612	          ))}
613	        </div>
614	
615	        {/* Your produce — inline list with quick suspend/resume */}
616	        <DashboardProduceSection
617	          listings={listings}
618	          onManage={() => setShowListings(true)}
619	          onToggleSuspend={handleToggleListingSuspend}
620	        />
621	
622	        {/* Monthly earnings summary */}
623	        <EarningsCard
624	          revenue={monthlyRevenue}
625	          orderCount={monthlyOrderCount}
626	          weekly={weeklyEarnings}
627	        />
628	
629	        {/* Today's pickups & deliveries */}
630	        {farmer && <TodayScheduleSection farmerId={farmer.id} />}
631	
632	        {/* Orders section */}
633	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
634	          <div className="px-4 pt-4 pb-3 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-gray-100">
635	            <div>
636	              <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
637	                {tx.ordersTab}
638	              </h2>
639	              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">Pending orders need your response</p>
640	            </div>
641	            <Link href="/farmer/dashboard/orders" className="text-xs font-bold text-green-700">
642	              {tx.viewOrderHistory}
643	            </Link>
644	          </div>
645	
646	          <div className="px-4 pt-3 pb-2 flex gap-2">
647	            {(['today', 'week', 'month'] as const).map((period) => (
648	              <button
649	                key={period}
650	                onClick={() => setOrdersFilter(period)}
651	                className={`px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
652	                  ordersFilter === period
653	                    ? 'bg-green-700 text-white'
654	                    : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-200'
655	                }`}
656	              >
657	                {period === 'today' ? tx.filterToday : period === 'week' ? tx.filterWeek : tx.filterMonth}
658	              </button>
659	            ))}
660	          </div>
661	
662	          <div className="px-4 pb-4 space-y-3">
663	            {filteredPendingOrders.length === 0 ? (
664	              <div className="text-center py-8">
665	                <div className="text-4xl mb-2">📭</div>
666	                <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noPendingOrders}</p>
667	                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{tx.noPendingHelp}</p>
668	              </div>
669	            ) : (
670	              filteredPendingOrders.map((order) => (
671	                <OrderCard
672	                  key={order.id}
673	                  order={order}
674	                  processing={processingOrderId === order.id}
675	                  processingPaid={processingPaidId === order.id}
676	                  onApprove={(date) => handleApprove(order.id, date)}
677	                  onDecline={() => setDecliningOrder(order)}
678	                  onMarkPaid={() => handleMarkPaid(order.id)}
679	                  onUpdatePaymentStatus={(s) => handleUpdatePaymentStatus(order.id, s)}
680	                  onSetFulfillmentDate={(d) => handleSetFulfillmentDate(order.id, d)}
681	                  onMarkPickedUp={() => handleMarkPickedUp(order.id)}
682	                  onMarkShipped={() => handleMarkShipped(order.id)}
683	                />
684	              ))
685	            )}
686	          </div>
687	        </div>
688	
689	        {/* Demand chart */}
690	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4">
691	          <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-base leading-tight">
692	            {tx.localDemand}
693	          </h2>
694	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 mb-4">
695	            {tx.localDemandHelp}
696	          </p>
697	
698	          {demandBars.length === 0 ? (
699	            <div className="text-center py-6">
700	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">{tx.noDemandSignals}</p>
701	              <p className="text-gray-400 text-xs mt-1">{tx.shareProfileLink}</p>
702	            </div>
703	          ) : (
704	            <div className="space-y-3">
705	              {demandBars.map((bar) => {
706	                const pct = Math.round((bar.total_qty / demandBars[0].total_qty) * 100)
707	                return (
708	                  <div key={bar.crop_name}>
709	                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-1">
710	                      <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-gray-800">{bar.crop_name}</span>
711	                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-medium">{bar.total_qty} kg</span>
712	                    </div>
713	                    <div className="h-3 bg-gray-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
714	                      <div
715	                        className="h-full bg-green-600 rounded-full"
716	                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
717	                      />
718	                    </div>
719	                  </div>
720	                )
721	              })}
722	            </div>
723	          )}
724	        </div>
725	
726	        {/* Add listing button */}
727	        {profileComplete ? (
728	          !showForm && (
729	            <button
730	              onClick={() => setShowForm(true)}
731	              className="w-full bg-white border-2 border-green-700 text-green-700 font-bold py-4 rounded-2xl text-base flex items-center justify-center gap-2 active:bg-green-50"
732	            >
733	              <span className="text-xl leading-none">+</span>
734	              {tx.addNewProduce}
735	            </button>
736	          )
737	        ) : (
738	          <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 font-semibold py-4 rounded-2xl text-sm text-center">
739	            {tx.completeBeforeAdd}
740	          </div>
741	        )}
742	
743	        {/* Listing form */}
744	        {showForm && profileComplete && (
745	          <ProduceListingForm
746	            farmerId={farmer!.id}
747	            farmerSlug={farmer!.slug}
748	            farmerRegion={farmer!.region_slug}
749	            defaultMethod={farmer!.method}
750	            onClose={() => setShowForm(false)}
751	            onPublished={() => { setShowForm(false); loadDashboard() }}
752	          />
753	        )}
754	
755	        {/* Farm photos */}
756	        {farmer && <FarmPhotosSection farmerId={farmer.id} />}
757	      </div>
758	
759	      {/* Manage listings modal */}
760	      {showListings && farmer && (
761	        <ManageListingsModal
762	          farmerId={farmer.id}
763	          farmerSlug={farmer.slug}
764	          farmerRegion={farmer.region_slug}
765	          defaultMethod={farmer.method ?? 'natural'}
766	          onClose={() => setShowListings(false)}
767	          onChanged={loadDashboard}
768	        />
769	      )}
770	
771	      {/* Edit profile modal */}
772	      {showProfileEdit && farmer && (
773	        <ProfileEditModal
774	          farmer={farmer}
775	          onClose={() => {
776	            // If profile still incomplete, don't allow close (keep banner as fallback)
777	            if (isProfileComplete(farmer)) setShowProfileEdit(false)
778	            else setShowProfileEdit(false) // allow dismiss; banner still shown
779	          }}
780	          onSaved={(updated) => {
781	            setFarmer(updated)
782	            setShowProfileEdit(false)
783	          }}
784	        />
785	      )}
786	
787	      {/* Decline reason sheet — mandatory reason capture */}
788	      {decliningOrder && (
789	        <DeclineReasonSheet
790	          order={decliningOrder}
791	          processing={processingOrderId === decliningOrder.id}
792	          onCancel={() => setDecliningOrder(null)}
793	          onConfirm={(reason) => handleConfirmDecline(decliningOrder.id, reason)}
794	        />
795	      )}
796	
797	      {/* Refund confirmation shown to the farmer right after declining */}
798	      {declineResult && (
799	        <DeclineSuccessSheet
800	          result={declineResult}
801	          onClose={() => setDeclineResult(null)}
802	        />
803	      )}
804	    </main>
805	  )
806	}

808	/* ─── Decline success / refund confirmation sheet ──────────── */
809	function DeclineSuccessSheet({
810	  result,
811	  onClose,
812	}: {
813	  result: { buyerName: string | null; amount: number | null; refundInitiated: boolean }
814	  onClose: () => void
815	}) {
816	  const buyer = result.buyerName || 'the customer'
817	  return (
818	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[130] bg-black/50 flex items-end sm:items-center justify-center p-0 sm:p-4">
819	      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl sm:rounded-3xl p-6 text-center space-y-3">
820	        <div className="text-4xl">✅</div>
821	        <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight">
822	          Order declined / ఆర్డర్ తిరస్కరించబడింది
823	        </h2>
824	
825	        {result.refundInitiated ? (
826	          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 text-left space-y-1.5">
827	            <p className="font-bold text-green-800 text-sm flex items-center gap-1.5">
828	              <span>💸</span> Refund initiated / రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభమైంది
829	            </p>
830	            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-snug">
831	              {result.amount != null && result.amount > 0 ? (
832	                <>A refund of <span className="font-extrabold text-green-800">₹{result.amount}</span> has been started to {buyer}.</>
833	              ) : (
834	                <>A refund has been started to {buyer}.</>
835	              )}
836	            </p>
837	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-snug">
838	              It will reach their account in <span className="font-semibold">3–5 business days</span>.
839	              The customer has been told this automatically.
840	              <br />
841	              కొనుగోలుదారు ఖాతాకు 3–5 పని దినాలలో జమ అవుతుంది.
842	            </p>
843	          </div>
844	        ) : (
845	          <div className="bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-2xl p-4 text-left">
846	            <p className="text-sm text-gray-700 leading-snug">
847	              {buyer} hadn&apos;t paid yet, so no refund is needed.
848	              <br />
849	              <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs">చెల్లింపు జరగలేదు, రీఫండ్ అవసరం లేదు.</span>
850	            </p>
851	          </div>
852	        )}
853	
854	        <button
855	          onClick={onClose}
856	          className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
857	        >
858	          Done / సరే
859	        </button>
860	      </div>
861	    </div>
862	  )
863	}
864	
865	/* ─── Decline reason bottom sheet ──────────────────────────── */
866	const DECLINE_PRESETS = [
867	  'Stock finished',
868	  'Not available this week',
869	  'Price changed',
870	  'Incorrect order details',
871	]
872	
873	function DeclineReasonSheet({
874	  order,
875	  processing,
876	  onCancel,
877	  onConfirm,
878	}: {
879	  order: Order
880	  processing: boolean
881	  onCancel: () => void
882	  onConfirm: (reason: string) => void
883	}) {
884	  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<string | null>(null)
885	  const [custom, setCustom] = useState('')
886	
887	  const finalReason = selected ?? custom.trim()
888	  const canSubmit = finalReason.length >= 3 && !processing
889	
890	  return (
891	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[120] bg-black/50 flex items-end justify-center">
892	      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-md rounded-t-3xl p-5 space-y-4">
893	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3">
894	          <div>
895	            <h2 className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-lg leading-tight">
896	              Why decline this order? / ఎందుకు తిరస్కరిస్తున్నారు?
897	            </h2>
898	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
899	              The reason will be shown to the buyer / కారణం కొనుగోలుదారుకు చూపబడుతుంది
900	            </p>
901	          </div>
902	          <button
903	            onClick={onCancel}
904	            disabled={processing}
905	            className="text-gray-400 text-3xl leading-none p-1 disabled:opacity-50"
906	          >
907	            ×
908	          </button>
909	        </div>
910	
911	        <div className="bg-gray-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-xs text-gray-700">
912	          <span className="font-semibold">{order.buyer_name || 'Buyer'}</span>
913	          {order.produce_name && <> · {order.produce_name}</>}
914	          {order.quantity != null && <> · {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</>}
915	        </div>
916	
917	        <div className="space-y-2">
918	          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
919	            Pick a reason / కారణం ఎంచుకోండి
920	          </p>
921	          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
922	            {DECLINE_PRESETS.map((preset) => (
923	              <button
924	                key={preset}
925	                type="button"
926	                onClick={() => { setSelected(preset); setCustom('') }}
927	                className={`px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold border-2 transition-colors text-left leading-snug ${
928	                  selected === preset
929	                    ? 'border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-800'
930	                    : 'border-gray-200 bg-white text-gray-700 active:bg-gray-50'
931	                }`}
932	              >
933	                {preset}
934	              </button>
935	            ))}
936	          </div>
937	        </div>
938	
939	        <div>
940	          <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-700 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1.5">
941	            Or type your reason / లేదా టైప్ చేయండి
942	          </label>
943	          <textarea
944	            value={custom}
945	            onChange={(e) => { setCustom(e.target.value); if (e.target.value.trim()) setSelected(null) }}
946	            placeholder="e.g. Heavy rain damaged the harvest"
947	            rows={2}
948	            className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none resize-none"
949	          />
950	        </div>
951	
952	        <div className="flex gap-2 pt-1">
953	          <button
954	            onClick={onCancel}
955	            disabled={processing}
956	            className="flex-1 border-2 border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-50"
957	          >
958	            Cancel / రద్దు
959	          </button>
960	          <button
961	            onClick={() => onConfirm(finalReason)}
962	            disabled={!canSubmit}
963	            className="flex-1 bg-red-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm disabled:opacity-50 active:bg-red-700"
964	          >
965	            {processing ? 'Declining...' : 'Confirm decline'}
966	          </button>
967	        </div>
968	      </div>
969	    </div>
970	  )
971	}
972	
973	/* ─── Notification permission banner ───────────────────────── */
974	function NotificationPermissionBanner() {
975	  const [perm, setPerm] = useState<'unsupported' | 'default' | 'granted' | 'denied'>('unsupported')
976	  const [dismissed, setDismissed] = useState(false)
977	
978	  useEffect(() => {
979	    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) {
980	      setPerm('unsupported')
981	      return
982	    }
983	    setPerm(Notification.permission as 'default' | 'granted' | 'denied')
984	    setDismissed(localStorage.getItem('yff_notif_banner_dismissed') === '1')
985	  }, [])
986	
987	  const handleEnable = async () => {
988	    if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) return
989	    const result = await Notification.requestPermission()
990	    setPerm(result)
991	    if (result === 'granted') {
992	      try { new Notification('YourFamilyFarmer', { body: 'You will be alerted on every new order.' }) } catch {}
993	    }
994	  }
995	
996	  const handleDismiss = () => {
997	    localStorage.setItem('yff_notif_banner_dismissed', '1')
998	    setDismissed(true)
999	  }
1000	
1001	  if (perm === 'unsupported' || perm === 'granted') return null
1002	  if (dismissed) return null
1003	
1004	  if (perm === 'denied') {
1005	    return (
1006	      <div className="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-start gap-3">
1007	        <span className="text-xl flex-shrink-0">🔕</span>
1008	        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
1009	          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-amber-900 leading-snug">
1010	            Notifications blocked / నోటిఫికేషన్‌లు బ్లాక్
1011	          </p>
1012	          <p className="text-[11px] text-amber-700 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
1013	            Enable notifications in your browser settings to get alerted on new orders.
1014	          </p>
1015	        </div>
1016	        <button onClick={handleDismiss} className="text-amber-700 text-xl leading-none px-1">×</button>
1017	      </div>
1018	    )
1019	  }
1020	
1021	  return (
1022	    <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-200 rounded-2xl p-3 flex items-start gap-3">
1023	      <span className="text-xl flex-shrink-0">🔔</span>
1024	      <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
1025	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-900 leading-snug">
1026	          Get notified instantly / తక్షణ నోటిఫికేషన్
1027	        </p>
1028	        <p className="text-[11px] text-blue-700 mt-0.5 leading-snug">
1029	          Allow notifications to be alerted the moment a buyer places an order.
1030	        </p>
1031	        <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
1032	          <button
1033	            onClick={handleEnable}
1034	            className="bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs active:bg-blue-700"
1035	          >
1036	            Enable / ఆన్ చేయండి
1037	          </button>
1038	          <button
1039	            onClick={handleDismiss}
1040	            className="border border-blue-300 text-blue-700 font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg text-xs"
1041	          >
1042	            Not now
1043	          </button>
1044	        </div>
1045	      </div>
1046	    </div>
1047	  )
1048	}
1049	
1050	/* ─── Profile edit modal ─────────────────────────────────── */
1051	function ProfileEditModal({
1052	  farmer,
1053	  onClose,
1054	  onSaved,
1055	}: {
1056	  farmer: Farmer
1057	  onClose: () => void
1058	  onSaved: (updated: Farmer) => void
1059	}) {
1060	  const { tx } = useLang()
1061	  const [name, setName] = useState(farmer.name ?? '')
1062	  const [village, setVillage] = useState(farmer.village ?? '')
1063	  const [district, setDistrict] = useState(farmer.district ?? '')
1064	  const [method, setMethod] = useState(farmer.method ?? 'natural')
1065	  const [sinceYear, setSinceYear] = useState(
1066	    farmer.farming_since_year ? String(farmer.farming_since_year) : '',
1067	  )
1068	  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>(
1069	    Array.isArray(farmer.pickup_locations) ? farmer.pickup_locations : [],
1070	  )
1071	  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')
1072	  const [farmAddress, setFarmAddress] = useState(farmer.farm_address ?? '')
1073	
1074	  // Cover photo
1075	  const [coverFile, setCoverFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
1076	  const [coverPreview, setCoverPreview] = useState('')
1077	  const [existingCoverUrl, setExistingCoverUrl] = useState(farmer.cover_photo_url ?? '')
1078	
1079	  // Avatar photo
1080	  const [avatarFile, setAvatarFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
1081	  const [avatarPreview, setAvatarPreview] = useState('')
1082	  const [existingAvatarUrl, setExistingAvatarUrl] = useState(farmer.photo_url ?? '')
1083	
1084	  // Pesticide cert
1085	  const [certFile, setCertFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
1086	  const [certPreview, setCertPreview] = useState('')
1087	  const [existingCertUrl, setExistingCertUrl] = useState(farmer.pesticide_cert_url ?? '')
1088	
1089	  // UPI ID + QR
1090	  const [upiId, setUpiId] = useState(farmer.upi_id ?? '')
1091	  const [qrFile, setQrFile] = useState<File | null>(null)
1092	  const [qrPreview, setQrPreview] = useState('')
1093	  const [existingQrUrl, setExistingQrUrl] = useState(farmer.upi_qr_code_url ?? '')
1094	
1095	  // Cash on Delivery acceptance — default off
1096	  const [codEnabled, setCodEnabled] = useState<boolean>(farmer.cod_enabled === true)
1097	
1098	  // Change password
1099	  const [showPwSection, setShowPwSection] = useState(false)
1100	  const [currentPassword, setCurrentPassword] = useState('')
1101	  const [newPassword, setNewPassword]     = useState('')
```
