210	
211	  const paymentLabel = (o: Order) => {
212	    if (o.payment_method === 'cod') return 'Cash on Delivery / నగదు చెల్లింపు'
213	    if (o.payment_method === 'upi') {
214	      // Show the real method (PhonePe / Google Pay / UPI…) once we know it,
215	      // instead of the gateway name. Falls back to "UPI" before it resolves.
216	      const m = o.payment_method_detail || 'UPI'
217	      if (o.payment_status === 'paid' || o.payment_status === 'completed') return `${m} ✓ Paid`
218	      if (o.payment_status === 'pending_confirmation' || o.payment_status === 'payment_claimed')
219	        return `${m} ⏳ Awaiting farmer confirmation`
220	      if (o.payment_status === 'failed') return `${m} ✕ Not received`
221	      return `${m} · Pending`
222	    }
223	    return o.payment_method ?? '—'
224	  }
225	
226	  const orderDate = order
227	    ? new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
228	        day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
229	      })
230	    : ''
231	  const orderTime = order
232	    ? new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleTimeString('en-IN', {
233	        hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
234	      })
235	    : ''
236	
237	  const whatsappHref = order?.farmer?.phone && order?.produce_name
238	    ? `https://wa.me/91${order.farmer.phone.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(-10)}?text=${encodeURIComponent(
239	        `Hello ${order.farmer.name} anna, regarding my order of ${order.produce_name} on ${orderDate}.`,
240	      )}`
241	    : null
242	
243	  return (
244	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
245	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
246	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
247	          <Link href="/consumer/orders" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1">
248	            ← {L('Back', 'వెనక్కు')}
249	          </Link>
250	          <LanguageToggle />
251	        </div>
252	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">
253	          {L('Order details', 'ఆర్డర్ వివరాలు')}
254	        </h1>
255	      </div>
256	
257	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4 max-w-lg mx-auto">
258	        {state.status === 'anonymous' ? (
259	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center space-y-4">
260	            <div className="text-5xl">🔒</div>
261	            <p className="font-bold text-gray-900">Log in to view this order</p>
262	            <button
263	              onClick={openAuth}
264	              className="w-full bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800"
265	            >
266	              {L('Log in', 'లాగిన్')}
267	            </button>
268	          </div>
269	        ) : loading ? (
270	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 text-center text-sm text-gray-500">
271	            {L('Loading...', 'లోడ్ అవుతోంది')}
272	          </div>
273	        ) : error ? (
274	          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-red-100 p-6 text-sm text-red-600">{error}</div>
275	        ) : !order ? null : (
276	          <>
277	            {/* Status + payment summary */}
278	            <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
279	              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
280	                <div>
281	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Order</p>
282	                  {order.order_code && (
283	                    <p className="text-[11px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400">{order.order_code}</p>
284	                  )}
285	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">
286	                    {order.produce_name || '—'}
287	                  </p>
288	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">
289	                    {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}
290	                    {order.total_price ? ` · ₹${order.total_price}` : ''}
291	                  </p>
292	                </div>
293	                <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full whitespace-nowrap ${statusColor(order.status)}`}>
294	                  {statusLabel(order.status)}
295	                </span>
296	              </div>
297	
298	              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-3">
299	                <div>
300	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Placed</p>
301	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{orderDate}</p>
302	                  <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">{orderTime}</p>
303	                </div>
304	                <div>
305	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Payment</p>
306	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">{paymentLabel(order)}</p>
307	                </div>
308	              </div>
309	
310	              {order.pickup_location && (
311	                <div className="pt-1 border-t border-gray-100 mt-1">
312	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Pickup</p>
313	                  <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-gray-700">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
314	                </div>
315	              )}
316	
317	              {(order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed') && (
318	                <button
319	                  onClick={() => setShowReceipt(true)}
320	                  className="mt-3 w-full border border-green-600 text-green-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-50"
321	                >
322	                  🧾 {L('View receipt', 'రసీదు చూడండి')}
323	                </button>
324	              )}
325	
326	              {canCancel && (
327	                <button
328	                  onClick={handleCancel}
329	                  disabled={cancelling}
330	                  className="mt-2 w-full border border-red-300 text-red-600 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-red-50 disabled:opacity-50"
331	                >
332	                  {cancelling ? 'Cancelling... / రద్దు చేస్తోంది...' : '✕ Cancel order / ఆర్డర్ రద్దు (30 min లోపు)'}
333	                </button>
334	              )}
335	
336	              {canConfirmReceipt && (
337	                <button
338	                  onClick={handleConfirmReceipt}
339	                  disabled={confirmingReceipt}
340	                  className="mt-3 w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 disabled:opacity-50"
341	                >
342	                  {confirmingReceipt ? '…' : '✓ Received / అందుకున్నాను'}
343	                </button>
344	              )}
345	            </div>
346	
347	            {/* Pickup / Delivery date the farmer scheduled for this order. */}
348	            {order.fulfillment_date && order.status !== 'declined' && order.status !== 'cancelled' && (
349	              <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center gap-3">
350	                <span className="text-2xl">📅</span>
351	                <div>
352	                  <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
353	                    {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
354	                      ? L('Delivery date', 'డెలివరీ తేదీ')
355	                      : L('Pickup date', 'పికప్ తేదీ')}
356	                  </p>
357	                  <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-900 mt-0.5">
358	                    {new Date(`${order.fulfillment_date}T00:00:00`).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
359	                      weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric',
360	                    })}
361	                  </p>
362	                </div>
363	              </div>
364	            )}
365	
366	            {/* Delivery timeline + handover OTP — only for home delivery orders */}
367	            {order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery' && order.status !== 'declined' && (
368	              <DeliveryPanel order={order} />
369	            )}
370	
371	            {/* Simple status timeline for self-pickup orders */}
372	            {order.delivery_type !== 'home_delivery' && order.status !== 'declined' && (
373	              <OrderStatusPanel order={order} />
374	            )}
375	
376	            {/* Decline reason */}
377	            {order.status === 'declined' && order.decline_reason && (
378	              <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4">
379	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-red-700 uppercase tracking-wide">Decline reason / కారణం</p>
380	                <p className="text-sm text-red-800 mt-1 leading-snug">{order.decline_reason}</p>
381	              </div>
382	            )}
383	
384	            {/* Refund status — shown whenever a refund exists for this order */}
385	            {order.refund_status && <RefundPanel order={order} />}
386	
387	            {/* Payment screenshot */}
388	            {order.payment_method === 'upi' && (
389	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-2">
390	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
391	                  Payment screenshot / చెల్లింపు స్క్రీన్‌షాట్
392	                </p>
393	                {!order.payment_proof_path ? (
394	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">No screenshot uploaded yet.</p>
395	                ) : proofLoading ? (
396	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Loading...</p>
397	                ) : proofUrl ? (
398	                  // eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element
399	                  <img
400	                    src={proofUrl}
401	                    alt="Payment screenshot"
402	                    className="w-full max-h-96 object-contain rounded-xl border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50"
403	                  />
404	                ) : (
405	                  <p className="text-xs text-red-600">Could not load screenshot. Please refresh.</p>
406	                )}
407	              </div>
408	            )}
409	
410	            {/* Farmer details */}
411	            {order.farmer && (
412	              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
413	                <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
414	                  Farmer / రైతు
415	                </p>
416	                <div>
417	                  <p className="text-base font-extrabold text-gray-900 leading-tight">🧑‍🌾 {order.farmer.name}</p>
418	                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-0.5">📍 {order.farmer.village}</p>
419	                </div>
420	                <Link
421	                  href={`/farmer/${order.farmer.slug}`}
422	                  className="block text-center text-sm font-bold text-green-700 underline"
423	                >
424	                  View farm profile / రైతు ప్రొఫైల్ చూడండి ↗
425	                </Link>
426	                {whatsappHref && (
427	                  <a
428	                    href={whatsappHref}
429	                    target="_blank"
430	                    rel="noopener noreferrer"
431	                    className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white font-bold py-3.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-700 flex items-center justify-center gap-2"
432	                  >
433	                    💬 {L('Contact Farmer', 'రైతును సంప్రదించు')}
434	                  </a>
435	                )}
436	              </div>
437	            )}
438	
439	            {/* Report a problem — pins the complaint to this order automatically */}
440	            <button
441	              onClick={() => setShowComplaint(true)}
442	              className="w-full border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50"
443	            >
444	              🛟 {L('Report a problem', 'సమస్యను నివేదించండి')}
445	            </button>
446	          </>
447	        )}
448	      </div>
449	
450	      {showReceipt && order && (
451	        <ReceiptOverlay order={order} onClose={() => setShowReceipt(false)} />
452	      )}
453	
454	      {showComplaint && order && (
455	        <ComplaintModal
456	          presetOrderCode={order.order_code}
457	          onClose={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
458	          onCreated={() => setShowComplaint(false)}
459	        />
460	      )}
461	    </main>
462	  )
463	}
464	
465	// Printable payment receipt. Shown as an overlay; the print stylesheet hides
466	// everything except .yff-receipt so "Print / Save as PDF" produces a clean
467	// one-page receipt on both phones and laptops.
468	function ReceiptOverlay({ order, onClose }: { order: Order; onClose: () => void }) {
469	  const placed = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', {
470	    day: 'numeric', month: 'short', year: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
471	  })
472	  const paymentRef = order.razorpay_payment_id || order.order_code || order.id
473	
474	  return (
475	    <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 overflow-y-auto">
476	      <style>{`@media print {
477	        body * { visibility: hidden !important; }
478	        .yff-receipt, .yff-receipt * { visibility: visible !important; }
479	        .yff-receipt { position: absolute; left: 0; top: 0; width: 100%; box-shadow: none !important; }
480	        .yff-no-print { display: none !important; }
481	      }`}</style>
482	
483	      <div className="yff-receipt bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-sm p-6 my-auto">
484	        <div className="text-center border-b border-dashed border-gray-300 pb-3">
485	          <p className="text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700">YourFamilyFarmer</p>
486	          <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-500">Payment Receipt / చెల్లింపు రసీదు</p>
487	        </div>
488	
489	        <div className="py-3 space-y-1.5 text-xs">
490	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Order ID / ఆర్డర్ ID</span><span className="font-mono font-bold text-gray-900">{order.order_code || order.id.slice(0, 8)}</span></div>
491	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Date / తేదీ</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{placed}</span></div>
492	          {order.farmer && (
493	            <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Farmer / రైతు</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900 text-right">{order.farmer.name} · {order.farmer.village}</span></div>
494	          )}
495	          <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Payment / చెల్లింపు</span><span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">{order.payment_method_detail || (order.payment_method === 'cod' ? 'Cash on Delivery' : order.payment_method === 'upi' ? 'UPI' : order.payment_method || '—')}</span></div>
496	        </div>
497	
498	        <div className="border-t border-dashed border-gray-300 py-3">
499	          <div className="flex justify-between text-xs">
500	            <span className="text-gray-700">{order.produce_name} × {order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>
501	            <span className="font-semibold text-gray-900">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>
502	          </div>
503	        </div>
504	
505	        <div className="border-t-2 border-gray-900 pt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
506	          <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900">Total Paid / మొత్తం చెల్లించారు</span>
507	          <span className="text-lg font-extrabold text-green-700">₹{order.total_price ?? 0}</span>
508	        </div>
509	
510	        <div className="mt-3 text-center">
511	          <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-800">✓ PAID / చెల్లించారు</span>
512	        </div>
513	
514	        <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 text-center mt-3 break-all">Ref: {paymentRef}</p>
515	        <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-green-700 text-center mt-2">Thank you for supporting our farmers — GoGrameen Team 🌱</p>
516	        <p className="text-[9px] text-gray-400 text-center mt-0.5">మా రైతులకు మద్దతు ఇచ్చినందుకు ధన్యవాదాలు — గోగ్రామీణ్ టీం</p>
517	
518	        <div className="yff-no-print mt-5 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
519	          <button onClick={onClose} className="border border-gray-300 text-gray-700 font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-gray-50">Close / మూసివేయి</button>
520	          <button onClick={() => window.print()} className="bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-2.5 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800">Print / ప్రింట్</button>
521	        </div>
522	      </div>
523	    </div>
524	  )
525	}
526	
527	function DeliveryPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
528	  const ds: DeliveryStatus = (order.delivery_status as DeliveryStatus) || 'unassigned'
529	
530	  const steps: Array<{ key: DeliveryStatus; label: string; sub: string; at: string | null | undefined }> = [
531	    { key: 'unassigned', label: 'Order placed', sub: 'Waiting for a delivery boy', at: order.created_at },
532	    { key: 'assigned', label: 'Rider assigned', sub: 'On the way to farmer', at: order.assigned_at },
533	    { key: 'picked_up', label: 'Picked up', sub: 'Collected from farmer', at: order.picked_up_at },
534	    { key: 'out_for_delivery', label: 'Out for delivery', sub: 'On the way to you', at: order.out_for_delivery_at },
535	    { key: 'delivered', label: 'Delivered', sub: 'Order completed', at: order.delivered_at },
536	  ]
537	
538	  const stageIndex = (k: DeliveryStatus) => ['unassigned', 'assigned', 'picked_up', 'out_for_delivery', 'delivered'].indexOf(k)
539	  const current = stageIndex(ds)
540	
541	  const formatAt = (iso: string | null | undefined) => {
542	    if (!iso) return ''
543	    const d = new Date(iso)
544	    return d.toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
545	  }
546	
547	  return (
548	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-4">
549	      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
550	        <span className="text-base">🛵</span>
551	        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">
552	          Home delivery / ఇంటికి డెలివరీ
553	        </p>
554	      </div>
555	
556	      {/* Handover OTP — visible once a rider has been assigned. Customer
557	          reads this 4-digit code to the rider at the door so the system
558	          can confirm the right person received the goods. */}
559	      {ds !== 'unassigned' && ds !== 'delivered' && order.handover_otp && (
560	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
561	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
562	            Read this code to the delivery boy at your door
563	          </p>
564	          <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 mt-0.5">
565	            డోర్ వద్ద డెలివరీ బాయ్‌కు ఈ కోడ్ చెప్పండి
566	          </p>
567	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
568	            {order.handover_otp}
569	          </p>
570	        </div>
571	      )}
572	
573	      {ds === 'delivered' && (
574	        <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
575	          <p className="text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800">✓ Delivered</p>
576	          <p className="text-xs text-green-700 mt-0.5">{formatAt(order.delivered_at)}</p>
577	        </div>
578	      )}
579	
580	      {/* Status timeline */}
581	      <ol className="space-y-2">
582	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
583	          const reached = idx <= current
584	          const isCurrent = idx === current
585	          return (
586	            <li key={s.key} className="flex items-start gap-3">
587	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
588	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
589	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
590	                  <span className={`w-0.5 flex-1 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
591	                )}
592	              </div>
593	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
594	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
595	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
596	                {s.at && reached && (
597	                  <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{formatAt(s.at)}</p>
598	                )}
599	              </div>
600	            </li>
601	          )
602	        })}
603	      </ol>
604	
605	      {/* Delivery address (always visible — confirms what consumer entered) */}
606	      {order.delivery_address && (
607	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
608	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide mb-1">Delivering to</p>
609	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-800 leading-snug whitespace-pre-line">{order.delivery_address}</p>
610	          {order.delivery_landmark && (
611	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 mt-0.5">📍 {order.delivery_landmark}</p>
612	          )}
613	          {order.delivery_pincode && (
614	            <p className="text-xs text-gray-600">PIN: {order.delivery_pincode}</p>
615	          )}
616	        </div>
617	      )}
618	
619	      {/* Rider contact — only when assigned (and not yet delivered) */}
620	      {order.rider && ds !== 'delivered' && (
621	        <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-3">
622	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wide">Delivery boy / డెలివరీ బాయ్</p>
623	          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-900 mt-1">{order.rider.name || 'Your delivery partner'}</p>
624	          <a
625	            href={`tel:${order.rider.phone}`}
626	            className="mt-2 w-full inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-blue-600 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-blue-700"
627	          >
628	            📞 Call · {order.rider.phone}
629	          </a>
630	        </div>
631	      )}
632	    </div>
633	  )
634	}
635	
636	// Order status timeline for self-pickup orders. Home delivery has its own
637	// richer DeliveryPanel; this covers the simpler pickup lifecycle.
638	function OrderStatusPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
639	  const approved = order.status === 'approved'
640	  const collected = !!order.collected_at
641	  // Online orders that have been paid show an extra "Payment received" step.
642	  const paidOnline = order.payment_method === 'upi'
643	    && (order.payment_status === 'paid' || order.payment_status === 'completed')
644	
645	  const fmt = (iso?: string | null) =>
646	    iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''
647	
648	  const steps = [
649	    { label: 'Order placed / ఆర్డర్ పెట్టారు', sub: 'We received your order / మీ ఆర్డర్ అందింది', at: fmt(order.created_at) },
650	    ...(paidOnline
651	      ? [{ label: 'Payment received / చెల్లింపు అందింది', sub: `${order.payment_method_detail || 'UPI'} payment confirmed / చెల్లింపు ధృవీకరించబడింది`, at: fmt(order.paid_at) }]
652	      : []),
653	    { label: 'Confirmed by farmer / రైతు ధృవీకరించారు', sub: 'Farmer accepted your order / రైతు అంగీకరించారు', at: fmt(order.confirmed_at) },
654	    { label: 'Ready for pickup / తీసుకెళ్లడానికి సిద్ధం', sub: order.pickup_location ? `Collect at ${order.pickup_location}` : 'Collect from the farmer / రైతు నుండి తీసుకోండి', at: '' },
655	    { label: 'Picked up / తీసుకున్నారు', sub: 'Collection confirmed / తీసుకున్నట్టు ధృవీకరించారు', at: fmt(order.collected_at) },
656	  ]
657	
658	  // current step index: placed(+payment) → confirmed/ready (on approval) → picked up.
659	  const lastIdx = steps.length - 1
660	  const readyIdx = lastIdx - 1
661	  const current = collected ? lastIdx : approved ? readyIdx : paidOnline ? 1 : 0
662	
663	  return (
664	    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-4 space-y-3">
665	      <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide">Order status / ఆర్డర్ స్థితి</p>
666	
667	      {/* Handover code — shown once the order is ready and before it's
668	          collected. The customer reads it to the farmer at pickup. */}
669	      {approved && !collected && order.handover_otp && (
670	        <div className="bg-amber-50 border-2 border-amber-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-3 text-center">
671	          <p className="text-[10px] font-bold text-amber-800 uppercase tracking-wide">
672	            Show this code to the farmer when you collect
673	          </p>
674	          <p className="text-[10px] text-amber-700 mt-0.5">
675	            తీసుకునేటప్పుడు ఈ కోడ్ రైతుకు చెప్పండి
676	          </p>
677	          <p className="text-4xl font-black tracking-widest text-amber-900 mt-2 font-mono">
678	            {order.handover_otp}
679	          </p>
680	        </div>
681	      )}
682	
683	      <ol className="space-y-2">
684	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
685	          const reached = idx <= current
686	          const isCurrent = idx === current
687	          return (
688	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
689	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
690	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-green-200' : ''}`} />
691	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
692	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-green-700' : 'bg-gray-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
693	                )}
694	              </div>
695	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
696	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-gray-900' : 'text-gray-400'}`}>{s.label}</p>
697	                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
698	                {s.at && reached && <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-0.5">{s.at}</p>}
699	              </div>
700	            </li>
701	          )
702	        })}
703	      </ol>
704	    </div>
705	  )
706	}
707	
708	// Refund timeline. Maps the stored refund_status (our manual 'initiated', or
709	// a Razorpay status: 'pending' / 'processed' / 'failed') onto a simple
710	// buyer-facing progression: Initiated → Processing → Credited.
711	function RefundPanel({ order }: { order: Order }) {
712	  const rs = (order.refund_status || '').toLowerCase()
713	  const failed = rs === 'failed'
714	  const amount = order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0
715	
716	  // current = how far along the 3-step bar we are.
717	  // initiated → 0, pending → 1, processed → 2 (credited).
718	  const current = rs === 'processed' ? 2 : rs === 'pending' ? 1 : 0
719	
720	  const steps = [
721	    { label: 'Refund initiated / రీఫండ్ మొదలైంది', sub: 'We started your refund / మీ రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభించాం' },
722	    { label: 'Processing / ప్రాసెస్ అవుతోంది', sub: 'Sent to your bank/UPI / మీ బ్యాంక్/UPIకి పంపాం' },
723	    { label: 'Credited / జమ అయింది', sub: 'Reflects in 3–5 business days / 3–5 పని రోజుల్లో కనిపిస్తుంది' },
724	  ]
725	
726	  const refundedAt = order.refunded_at
727	    ? new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })
728	    : null
729	
730	  if (failed) {
731	    return (
732	      <div className="bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-1.5">
733	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-800">
734	          ⚠️ Refund failed / రీఫండ్ విఫలమైంది
735	        </span>
736	        <p className="text-sm text-red-800 leading-snug">
737	          Your refund of ₹{amount} could not be processed. Please contact support and we&apos;ll fix it.
738	        </p>
739	        <p className="text-sm text-red-800 leading-snug">
740	          మీ ₹{amount} రీఫండ్ ప్రాసెస్ కాలేదు. దయచేసి సపోర్ట్‌ను సంప్రదించండి, మేము సరి చేస్తాం.
741	        </p>
742	      </div>
743	    )
744	  }
745	
746	  return (
747	    <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-200 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3">
748	      <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
749	        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-100 text-purple-800">
750	          💸 Refund of ₹{amount}
751	        </span>
752	        {refundedAt && <span className="text-[10px] text-purple-500">{refundedAt}</span>}
753	      </div>
754	
755	      <ol className="space-y-2">
756	        {steps.map((s, idx) => {
757	          const reached = idx <= current
758	          const isCurrent = idx === current
759	          return (
760	            <li key={s.label} className="flex items-start gap-3">
761	              <div className="flex flex-col items-center pt-0.5">
762	                <span className={`w-3 h-3 rounded-full ${reached ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'} ${isCurrent ? 'ring-2 ring-purple-200' : ''}`} />
763	                {idx < steps.length - 1 && (
764	                  <span className={`w-0.5 mt-0.5 ${idx < current ? 'bg-purple-600' : 'bg-purple-200'}`} style={{ minHeight: 14 }} />
765	                )}
766	              </div>
767	              <div className="flex-1 pb-1">
768	                <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${reached ? 'text-purple-900' : 'text-purple-300'}`}>{s.label}</p>
769	                <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-500 leading-snug">{s.sub}</p>
770	              </div>
771	            </li>
772	          )
773	        })}
774	      </ol>
775	
776	      {order.refund_id && (
777	        <p className="text-[10px] text-purple-400 font-mono border-t border-purple-100 pt-2">Ref: {order.refund_id}</p>
778	      )}
779	    </div>
780	  )
781	}
782

3	import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
4	import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
5	import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
6	import Link from 'next/link'
7	import { useLang } from '@/lib/LanguageContext'
8	
9	type Order = {
10	  id: string
11	  farmer_id: string
12	  order_code: string | null
13	  produce_listing_id: string | null
14	  produce_name: string | null
15	  quantity: number | null
16	  unit: string | null
17	  total_price: number | null
18	  buyer_name: string | null
19	  buyer_phone: string | null
20	  pickup_location: string | null
21	  status: 'pending' | 'approved' | 'declined'
22	  payment_status: string | null
23	  decline_reason: string | null
24	  refund_status: string | null
25	  refund_amount: number | null
26	  refunded_at: string | null
27	  delivery_type: 'self_pickup' | 'home_delivery' | null
28	  collected_at: string | null
29	  fulfillment_date: string | null
30	  created_at: string
31	}
32	
33	type Filter = 'today' | 'week' | 'month'
34	
35	export default function OrderHistoryPage() {
36	  const router = useRouter()
37	  const { tx } = useLang()
38	  const [orders, setOrders] = useState<Order[]>([])
39	  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
40	  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>('week')
41	
42	  const load = useCallback(async () => {
43	    const farmerId = localStorage.getItem('yff_farmer_id')
44	    if (!farmerId) { router.replace('/farmer/login'); return }
45	
46	    setLoading(true)
47	    // Explicit columns: handover_otp is deliberately NOT fetched — the pickup
48	    // code must come from the customer, so the farmer's browser never sees it.
49	    const { data } = await supabase
50	      .from('orders')
51	      .select('id, farmer_id, order_code, produce_listing_id, produce_name, quantity, unit, total_price, buyer_name, buyer_phone, pickup_location, status, payment_status, decline_reason, refund_status, refund_amount, refunded_at, delivery_type, collected_at, fulfillment_date, created_at')
52	      .eq('farmer_id', farmerId)
53	      .in('status', ['approved', 'declined'])
54	      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
55	
56	    setOrders((data ?? []) as Order[])
57	    setLoading(false)
58	  }, [router])
59	
60	  useEffect(() => { load() }, [load])
61	
62	  // Farmer sets/updates the pickup-or-delivery date on an approved order.
63	  const setFulfillmentDate = async (orderId: string, date: string) => {
64	    const value = date || null
65	    setOrders((prev) => prev.map((o) => (o.id === orderId ? { ...o, fulfillment_date: value } : o)))
66	    await supabase.from('orders').update({ fulfillment_date: value }).eq('id', orderId)
67	  }
68	
69	  const filterStart = () => {
70	    if (filter === 'today') {
71	      const d = new Date()
72	      d.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
73	      return d.getTime()
74	    }
75	    if (filter === 'week') return Date.now() - 7 * 86400000
76	    return Date.now() - 30 * 86400000
77	  }
78	
79	  const filtered = orders.filter((o) => new Date(o.created_at).getTime() >= filterStart())
80	  const revenue = filtered
81	    .filter((o) => o.status === 'approved')
82	    .reduce((sum, o) => sum + (o.total_price ?? 0), 0)
83	
84	  return (
85	    <main className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 pb-16">
86	      {/* Header */}
87	      <div className="bg-green-900 px-4 pt-6 pb-10">
88	        <Link href="/farmer/dashboard" className="text-green-300 text-sm flex items-center gap-1 mb-4">
89	          ← {tx.back}
90	        </Link>
91	        <h1 className="text-white text-xl font-extrabold leading-tight">
92	          {tx.orderHistory}
93	        </h1>
94	        <p className="text-green-400 text-sm mt-1">Approved &amp; declined orders</p>
95	      </div>
96	
97	      <div className="px-4 -mt-5 space-y-4">
98	        {/* Filter pills */}
99	        <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-3 flex gap-2">
100	          {(['today', 'week', 'month'] as const).map((f) => (
101	            <button
102	              key={f}
103	              onClick={() => setFilter(f)}
104	              className={`flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-colors ${
105	                filter === f ? 'bg-green-700 text-white' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 active:bg-gray-200'
106	              }`}
107	            >
108	              {f === 'today' ? tx.filterToday : f === 'week' ? tx.filterWeek : tx.filterMonth}
109	            </button>
110	          ))}
111	        </div>
112	
113	        {/* Revenue summary */}
114	        {revenue > 0 && (
115	          <div className="bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-2xl p-4 flex items-center justify-between">
116	            <div>
117	              <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700 uppercase tracking-wide">
118	                {tx.totalRevenue}
119	              </p>
120	              <p className="text-3xl font-black text-green-800 mt-0.5">₹{revenue}</p>
121	            </div>
122	            <div className="text-4xl">💰</div>
123	          </div>
124	        )}
125	
126	        {/* Order list */}
127	        {loading ? (
128	          <div className="text-center py-16">
129	            <div className="w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin mx-auto" />
130	            <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm mt-3">{tx.loadingLabel}</p>
131	          </div>
132	        ) : filtered.length === 0 ? (
133	          <div className="text-center py-16">
134	            <div className="text-5xl mb-3">📭</div>
135	            <p className="font-semibold text-gray-500 text-sm">{tx.noPendingOrders}</p>
136	          </div>
137	        ) : (
138	          <div className="space-y-3">
139	            {filtered.map((order) => (
140	              <HistoryCard
141	                key={order.id}
142	                order={order}
143	                onSetDate={(d) => setFulfillmentDate(order.id, d)}
144	              />
145	            ))}
146	          </div>
147	        )}
148	      </div>
149	    </main>
150	  )
151	}
152	
153	function HistoryCard({ order, onSetDate }: { order: Order; onSetDate: (date: string) => void }) {
154	  const { tx } = useLang()
155	  const isApproved = order.status === 'approved'
156	  const isDelivery = order.delivery_type === 'home_delivery'
157	
158	  const timeStr = new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', {
159	    day: 'numeric',
160	    month: 'short',
161	    hour: '2-digit',
162	    minute: '2-digit',
163	  })
164	
165	  return (
166	    <div className={`rounded-2xl border overflow-hidden ${isApproved ? 'border-green-200 bg-white' : 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50'}`}>
167	      <div className="p-3 space-y-1.5">
168	        <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
169	          <div className="min-w-0">
170	            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
171	              <p className="font-extrabold text-gray-900 text-sm leading-tight">
172	                {order.buyer_name || '—'}
173	              </p>
174	              <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
175	                isApproved ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-800' : 'bg-red-100 text-red-700'
176	              }`}>
177	                {isApproved ? tx.statusApproved : tx.statusDeclined}
178	              </span>
179	            </div>
180	            {order.buyer_phone && (
181	              <a href={`tel:+91${order.buyer_phone}`} className="text-xs font-semibold text-green-700">
182	                📞 +91 {order.buyer_phone}
183	              </a>
184	            )}
185	          </div>
186	          <div className="flex flex-col items-end flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5">
187	            <span className="text-[11px] text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
188	              {timeStr}
189	            </span>
190	            {order.order_code && (
191	              <span className="text-[10px] font-mono font-semibold text-gray-400 whitespace-nowrap">
192	                {order.order_code}
193	              </span>
194	            )}
195	          </div>
196	        </div>
197	
198	        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 text-sm">
199	          <span className="font-semibold text-gray-800">{order.produce_name || '—'}</span>
200	          <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
201	          <span className="text-gray-600">{order.quantity} {order.unit || 'kg'}</span>
202	          {order.total_price != null && order.total_price > 0 && (
203	            <>
204	              <span className="text-gray-300">·</span>
205	              <span className="font-bold text-green-700">₹{order.total_price}</span>
206	            </>
207	          )}
208	        </div>
209	
210	        {order.pickup_location && (
211	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">📍 {order.pickup_location}</p>
212	        )}
213	
214	        {/* Approved orders: farmer sets/edits the pickup-or-delivery date. */}
215	        {isApproved && (
216	          <div className="pt-1">
217	            <label className="text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-600 block mb-1">
218	              📅 {isDelivery ? tx.deliveryDateLabel : tx.pickupDateLabel}
219	            </label>
220	            <input
221	              type="date"
222	              value={order.fulfillment_date ?? ''}
223	              onChange={(e) => onSetDate(e.target.value)}
224	              className="w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 text-sm bg-white focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none"
225	            />
226	          </div>
227	        )}
228	
229	        {/* Declined orders: show the reason and the refund status to the farmer */}
230	        {!isApproved && order.decline_reason && (
231	          <p className="text-xs text-gray-600 leading-snug">
232	            <span className="font-semibold">Reason / కారణం:</span> {order.decline_reason}
233	          </p>
234	        )}
235	        {!isApproved && (order.refund_status || (order.refund_amount ?? 0) > 0) && (
236	          <div className="mt-1 bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5">
237	            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-green-800 flex items-center gap-1">
238	              💸 Refund initiated to customer / రీఫండ్ ప్రారంభమైంది
239	            </p>
240	            <p className="text-[11px] text-gray-600 leading-snug mt-0.5">
241	              ₹{order.refund_amount ?? order.total_price ?? 0} · reflects in 3–5 business days
242	              {order.refunded_at && (
243	                <> · {new Date(order.refunded_at).toLocaleDateString('en-IN', { day: 'numeric', month: 'short' })}</>
244	              )}
245	            </p>
246	          </div>
247	        )}
248	      </div>
249	    </div>
250	  )
251	}

> AGENT

I now have the complete picture. The recovered commit delivered the core flow, but I found genuine gaps against your spec. Here's my reconciliation:

**Already done (recovered commit):** farmer "Picked Up" + "Shipped" buttons (exact bilingual labels), courier stays active until received, consumer "Received" button, `shipped_at`/`received_at` columns, farmer realtime notifications, consumer 25s polling.

**Gaps I'll now close:**
1. **Consumer courier timeline is broken** — `OrderStatusPanel` shows *pickup* steps ("Ready for pickup → Picked up" via `collected_at`) for courier orders too, so a courier buyer sees a timeline that never completes. It needs Shipped → Received steps with dates.
2. **No consumer notification** when farmer marks Shipped (only silent polling).
3. **No farmer notification** when consumer marks Received.
4. **Farmer order-history** doesn't show courier shipped/received completion dates.

One spec deviation I'm keeping deliberately: you listed a new **`picked_up_at`** column, but that column **already exists and is used by the rider/home-delivery flow** ("rider picked up from farmer"). Reusing it for consumer self-pickup would corrupt the rider timeline, so the recovered code correctly uses `collected_at` for pickup completion. I'll keep `collected_at` and flag this.

Let me implement the four fixes.

{
  "replace_all": false,

  "old_string": "type DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'",
  "new_string": "type DeliveryStatus = 'unassigned' | 'assigned' | 'picked_up' | 'out_for_delivery' | 'delivered'\n\n// Fire a browser notification for a buyer-facing status change (e.g. the farmer\n// shipped the parcel). Permission-gated: silently no-ops unless the buyer has\n// granted notifications, so it's safe to call unconditionally.\nfunction fireConsumerNotification(title: string, body: string) {\n  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('Notification' in window)) return\n  if (Notification.permission !== 'granted') return\n  try {\n    new Notification(title, { body, icon: '/icon-192.png', tag: 'yff-order-status' })\n  } catch { /* some browsers throw on background tabs — ignore */ }\n}"
}

> AGENT

Build passes (exit 0). All four gaps are closed. The full order-completion flow now matches your spec end-to-end.

## What I added this session (on top of the recovered base)

**1. Consumer courier timeline — fixed (was the real bug)**
`OrderStatusPanel` was showing *pickup* steps for courier orders, so a courier buyer saw a timeline that could never complete. It's now delivery-type aware:
- **Pickup:** Placed → (Payment) → Confirmed → Ready for pickup → **Picked up** (`collected_at`)
- **Courier:** Placed → (Payment) → Confirmed → **Shipped** (`shipped_at`) → **Received** (`received_at`)

Every step shows its real date. Telugu labels throughout (`షిప్ చేయబడింది`, `అందుకున్నారు`, etc.).

**2. Consumer notification on "Shipped"** — the 25s poll now fires a browser notification (`Your order has shipped 📦 / ఆర్డర్ షిప్ చేయబడింది`) the moment the farmer ships, in addition to the live in-page update.

**3. Farmer notification on "Received"** — the dashboard realtime channel now alerts the farmer (`Order received ✓ / అందుకున్నారు`) right before the order moves to history.

**4. Farmer order-history completion line** — history cards now show the final milestone + date: `✓ Picked up`, `📦 Shipped`, or `✓ Received` with timestamps (added `shipped_at`/`received_at` to the query + `courier` type).

## Two things to flag

- **`picked_up_at` column:** I did **not** add it. That column already exists and means *"rider collected from farmer"* in the home-delivery flow — reusing it for consumer self-pickup would corrupt the rider timeline. Pickup completion correctly uses `collected_at` instead. Net result is identical for your spec; just a safer column choice.
- **Consumer push notifications are permission-gated** and there's no consumer-side "enable notifications" prompt yet (farmers have one). So for buyers, the **live in-page timeline update** is the reliable mechanism; the browser push only fires where a buyer has already granted permission (e.g. installed PWA). Adding a consumer notification banner would be a small follow-up if you want guaranteed push.

Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean and `npm run build` succeeds.

These changes are **uncommitted** on top of `93f77c3`. Want me to fold them into a follow-up commit (`feat(orders): courier-aware buyer timeline + status-change notifications`)? I won't push without your OK.

11	// Shrink large camera photos before upload so they stay quick on a 4G connection.
12	async function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {
13	  return new Promise((resolve) => {
14	    const img = new Image()
15	    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)
16	    img.onload = () => {
17	      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)
18	      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))
19	      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')
20	      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)
21	      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)
22	      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')
23	      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }
24	      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)
25	      canvas.toBlob(
26	        (blob) => {
27	          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }
28	          const name = file.name.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')
29	          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))
30	        },
31	        'image/jpeg',
32	        quality,
33	      )
34	    }
35	    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }
36	    img.src = blobUrl
37	  })
38	}
39

85	    const file = e.target.files?.[0]

397	function Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {

402	    </label>
403	  )
404	}
405	
406	function PhotoUpload({
407	  preview,
408	  onPick,
409	  onClear,
410	  aspectClass,
411	}: {
412	  preview: string
413	  onPick: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void
414	  onClear: () => void
415	  aspectClass: string
416	}) {
417	  if (preview) {
418	    return (
419	      <div className={`relative ${aspectClass} rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50`}>
420	        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}
421	        <img src={preview} alt="" className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
422	        <button
423	          type="button"
424	          onClick={onClear}
425	          className="absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 text-gray-700 rounded-full w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold shadow"
426	        >
427	          ×
428	        </button>
429	      </div>
430	    )
431	  }
432	  return (
433	    <label className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-2 text-green-700 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50">
434	      <span>📷</span> Add photo
435	      <input type="file" accept="image/*" onChange={onPick} className="hidden" />
436	    </label>
437	  )
438	}

1	import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
2	import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone } from '@/lib/moderator-session'
4	
5	export const runtime = 'nodejs'
6	export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'
7	
8	const METHODS = ['natural', 'organic', 'low_chemical', 'chemical'] as const
9	
10	function slugify(name: string): string {
11	  return name.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, '')
12	}
13	
14	function svc() {
15	  return createClient(
16	    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
17	    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
18	  )
19	}
20	
21	// GET — all farmers in the moderator's zone, with a live listing count.
22	export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
23	  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
24	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
25	  }
26	  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
27	  const supabase = svc()
28	
29	  const { data: farmers, error } = await supabase
30	    .from('farmers')
31	    .select('id, slug, name, village, district, method, phone, active, created_at')
32	    .eq('region_slug', zone)
33	    .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
34	
35	  if (error) {
36	    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers] query failed:', error.message)
37	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
38	  }
39	
40	  // Listing count per farmer (one grouped query, merged in JS).
41	  const ids = (farmers ?? []).map((f) => f.id)
42	  const counts: Record<string, number> = {}
43	  if (ids.length > 0) {
44	    const { data: listings } = await supabase
45	      .from('produce_listings')
46	      .select('farmer_id')
47	      .in('farmer_id', ids)
48	    for (const l of listings ?? []) {
49	      if (l.farmer_id) counts[l.farmer_id] = (counts[l.farmer_id] ?? 0) + 1
50	    }
51	  }
52	
53	  const withCounts = (farmers ?? []).map((f) => ({ ...f, listing_count: counts[f.id] ?? 0 }))
54	  return NextResponse.json({ farmers: withCounts })
55	}
56	
57	// POST — register a new farmer in the zone. Auto-generates a unique slug.
58	export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
59	  if (!isModeratorRequest(req)) {
60	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Moderator login required.' }, { status: 401 })
61	  }
62	  const zone = getModeratorZone(req)
63	  const supabase = svc()
64	
65	  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
66	  if (!body) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid body.' }, { status: 400 })
67	
68	  const name = String((body as { name?: unknown }).name ?? '').trim()
69	  const phone = String((body as { phone?: unknown }).phone ?? '').trim()
70	  const village = String((body as { village?: unknown }).village ?? '').trim()
71	  const district = String((body as { district?: unknown }).district ?? '').trim()
72	  const methodRaw = String((body as { method?: unknown }).method ?? 'natural').trim()
73	  const story_quote = String((body as { story_quote?: unknown }).story_quote ?? '').trim()
74	  const farm_size_acres = Number((body as { farm_size_acres?: unknown }).farm_size_acres ?? 0) || null
75	  const farming_since_year = Number((body as { farming_since_year?: unknown }).farming_since_year ?? 0) || null
76	
77	  // Marketing / payout details — the same things a farmer fills on their own
78	  // profile, so a moderator onboarding on their behalf can capture them up front.
79	  const farm_address = String((body as { farm_address?: unknown }).farm_address ?? '').trim()
80	  const upi_id = String((body as { upi_id?: unknown }).upi_id ?? '').trim()
81	  const cod_enabled = (body as { cod_enabled?: unknown }).cod_enabled === true
82	
83	  // Photos & farm GPS — the same media a farmer can attach to their own profile.
84	  const cover_photo_url = String((body as { cover_photo_url?: unknown }).cover_photo_url ?? '').trim()
85	  const photo_url = String((body as { photo_url?: unknown }).photo_url ?? '').trim()
86	  const pesticide_cert_url = String((body as { pesticide_cert_url?: unknown }).pesticide_cert_url ?? '').trim()
87	  const upi_qr_code_url = String((body as { upi_qr_code_url?: unknown }).upi_qr_code_url ?? '').trim()
88	  const location_name = String((body as { location_name?: unknown }).location_name ?? '').trim()
89	  const latRaw = (body as { lat?: unknown }).lat
90	  const lngRaw = (body as { lng?: unknown }).lng
91	  const lat = typeof latRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(latRaw) ? latRaw : null
92	  const lng = typeof lngRaw === 'number' && Number.isFinite(lngRaw) ? lngRaw : null
93	
94	  const rawPickups = (body as { pickup_locations?: unknown }).pickup_locations
95	  const pickup_locations = Array.isArray(rawPickups)
96	    ? Array.from(new Set(rawPickups.map((p) => String(p).trim()).filter(Boolean)))
97	    : []
98	
99	  // pickup_slots: { days: string[], time_from, time_to } — stored only when at
100	  // least one day is chosen, matching the farmer-side profile editor.
101	  const slots = (body as { pickup_slots?: unknown }).pickup_slots as
102	    | { days?: unknown; time_from?: unknown; time_to?: unknown }
103	    | null
104	    | undefined
105	  const slotDays = Array.isArray(slots?.days) ? slots!.days.map((d) => String(d)).filter(Boolean) : []
106	  const pickup_slots = slotDays.length > 0
107	    ? { days: slotDays, time_from: String(slots?.time_from ?? '08:00'), time_to: String(slots?.time_to ?? '12:00') }
108	    : null
109	
110	  if (!name) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Name is required.' }, { status: 400 })
111	  if (upi_id && !/^[a-zA-Z0-9._-]{2,256}@[a-zA-Z]{2,64}$/.test(upi_id)) {
112	    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid UPI ID. Example: name@ybl' }, { status: 400 })
113	  }
114	  const method = (METHODS as readonly string[]).includes(methodRaw) ? methodRaw : 'natural'
115	
116	  // Unique slug — append -2, -3, ... if taken.
117	  const base = slugify(name) || 'farmer'
118	  let slug = base
119	  for (let i = 2; i < 50; i++) {
120	    const { data: existing } = await supabase.from('farmers').select('id').eq('slug', slug).maybeSingle()
121	    if (!existing) break
122	    slug = `${base}-${i}`
123	  }
124	
125	  const { data: inserted, error } = await supabase
126	    .from('farmers')
127	    .insert({
128	      slug,
129	      name,
130	      phone: phone || null,
131	      village: village || null,
132	      district: district || null,
133	      method,
134	      story_quote: story_quote || null,
135	      farm_size_acres,
136	      farming_since_year,
137	      farm_address: farm_address || null,
138	      pickup_locations,
139	      pickup_slots,
140	      upi_id: upi_id || null,
141	      upi_qr_code_url: upi_qr_code_url || null,
142	      cod_enabled,
143	      cover_photo_url: cover_photo_url || null,
144	      photo_url: photo_url || null,
145	      pesticide_cert_url: pesticide_cert_url || null,
146	      lat,
147	      lng,
148	      location_name: lat != null && lng != null ? (location_name || name) : null,
149	      region_slug: zone,
150	      active: true,
151	    })
152	    .select('id, slug, name, phone')
153	    .single()
154	
155	  if (error) {
156	    console.error('[YFF moderator/farmers] insert failed:', error.message)
157	    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 })
158	  }
159	
160	  return NextResponse.json({ farmer: inserted })
161	}
162

1	import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'
2	import type { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	import { getSessionSecret } from '@/lib/session'
4	
5	// Moderator session. Like the owner/admin panel, access is gated by a single
6	// MODERATOR_PASSWORD env var — there is no moderator user table (for the first
7	// 3 months the founders themselves are the moderator). Successful password
8	// check sets this cookie; every moderator endpoint verifies it. The HMAC is
9	// namespaced with `moderator:` so a consumer, farmer, rider, or admin token
10	// cannot be replayed against moderator routes — that is what keeps the
11	// /moderator area invisible to every other role.
12	const COOKIE_NAME = 'yff_moderator'
13	const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000
14	
15	function b64url(buf: Buffer): string {
16	  return buf.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')
17	}
18	
19	function sign(payload: string): string {
20	  const secret=[REDACTED]()
21	  return b64url(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`moderator:${payload}`).digest())
22	}
23	
24	export function getModeratorPassword(): string {
25	  const pw = process.env.MODERATOR_PASSWORD
26	  if (!pw || pw.length < 8) {
27	    throw new Error(
28	      'MODERATOR_PASSWORD env var is missing or too short. Set an 8+ char value in your environment.',
29	    )
30	  }
31	  return pw
32	}
33	
34	// The zone the request's moderator manages. Read from their signed session
35	// token (stamped with their own region_slug at login), so moderators of
36	// different zones can all use one deployment and each stays scoped to their
37	// own region. Falls back to the MODERATOR_ZONE env var when there's no
38	// moderator session on the request (e.g. routing complaints for other roles).
39	export function getModeratorZone(req?: NextRequest): string {
40	  if (req) {
41	    const t = readModeratorToken(req)
42	    if (t?.zone) return t.zone
43	  }
44	  return process.env.MODERATOR_ZONE || 'tadepalligudem'
45	}
46	
47	export function createModeratorSessionToken(zone: string): string {
48	  const issuedAt = Date.now()
49	  const zoneB64 = b64url(Buffer.from(zone, 'utf8'))
50	  const payload = `moderator.${issuedAt}.${zoneB64}`
51	  return `${payload}.${sign(payload)}`
52	}
53	
54	// Parse + verify the moderator cookie. Returns the decoded zone (and issue
55	// time) when valid, or null. Token shape: moderator.<issuedAt>.<zoneB64>.<sig>
56	function readModeratorToken(req: NextRequest): { issuedAt: number; zone: string } | null {
57	  const token = req.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value
58	  if (!token) return null
59	  const parts = token.split('.')
60	  if (parts.length !== 4) return null
61	  const [marker, issuedAtStr, zoneB64, sig] = parts
62	  if (marker !== 'moderator') return null
63	  const issuedAt = Number(issuedAtStr)
64	  if (!Number.isFinite(issuedAt)) return null
65	  if (Date.now() - issuedAt > TOKEN_TTL_MS) return null
66	  let expected: string
67	  try {
68	    expected = sign(`${marker}.${issuedAtStr}.${zoneB64}`)
69	  } catch {
70	    return null
71	  }
72	  const a = Buffer.from(sig)
73	  const b = Buffer.from(expected)
74	  if (a.length !== b.length) return null
75	  if (!timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null
76	  let zone = ''
77	  try {
78	    zone = Buffer.from(zoneB64.replace(/-/g, '+').replace(/_/g, '/'), 'base64').toString('utf8')
79	  } catch {
80	    return null
81	  }
82	  return { issuedAt, zone }
83	}
84	
85	export function isModeratorRequest(req: NextRequest): boolean {
86	  return readModeratorToken(req) !== null
87	}
88	
89	export function setModeratorSessionCookie(res: NextResponse, zone: string): void {
90	  const token=[REDACTED](zone)
91	  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, token, {
92	    httpOnly: true,
93	    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
94	    sameSite: 'lax',
95	    path: '/',
96	    maxAge: Math.floor(TOKEN_TTL_MS / 1000),
97	  })
98	}
99	
100	export function clearModeratorSessionCookie(res: NextResponse): void {
101	  res.cookies.set(COOKIE_NAME, '', {
102	    httpOnly: true,
103	    secure: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production',
104	    sameSite: 'lax',
105	    path: '/',
106	    maxAge: 0,
107	  })
108	}
109	
110	export const MODERATOR_SESSION_COOKIE_NAME = COOKIE_NAME
111

=== moderator login route ===
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { setModeratorSessionCookie } from '@/lib/moderator-session'
import { verifyPassword } from '@/lib/password'
import { normalizePhone } from '@/lib/phone'
import { rateLimit } from '@/lib/rate-limit'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

// Phone + password login, same shape as the farmer/consumer login. Credentials
// live in the `moderators` table (scrypt-hashed). A moderator can only sign in
// to the zone they're assigned to (region_slug must match this panel's zone).
export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const body = await req.json().catch(() => null)
  const phone = normalizePhone((body as { phone?: unknown })?.phone as string)
  const password = String((body as { password?: unknown })?.password ?? '')

  if (!phone) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid 10-digit phone number.' }, { status: 400 })
  }
  if (!password) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Password required.' }, { status: 400 })
  }

  // Brute-force throttle: 5 per phone / 30 per ip in 15 min.
  const ip = req.headers.get('x-forwarded-for')?.split(',')[0]?.trim() || 'unknown'
  if (
    !rateLimit(`moderator-login:phone:${phone}`, 5, 15 * 60 * 1000) ||
    !rateLimit(`moderator-login:ip:${ip}`, 30, 15 * 60 * 1000)
  ) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Too many attempts. Wait 15 min.' }, { status: 429 })
  }

  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,
  )

  const { data: mods } = await supabase
    .from('moderators')
    .select('id, phone, password_hash, region_slug, active')
    .or([`phone.eq.${phone}`, `phone.eq.0${phone}`, `phone.eq.+91${phone}`, `phone.eq.91${phone}`].join(','))
    .limit(1)
  const mod = mods?.[0]

  // Anti-enumeration: identical error whether the phone exists or not.
  const wrongCreds = NextResponse.json({ error: 'Wrong phone or password.' }, { status: 401 })

  if (!mod || !mod.password_hash) return wrongCreds
  if (!verifyPassword(password, mod.password_hash)) return wrongCreds
  if (mod.active === false) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'This moderator account is inactive.' }, { status: 403 })
  }

  // No global-zone gate: each moderator's own region_slug is carried in their
  // session, so any zone's moderator can sign in here and stays scoped to
  // their region across the panel.
  const res = NextResponse.json({ ok: true, zone: mod.region_slug })
  setModeratorSessionCookie(res, mod.region_slug)
  return res
}

=== /api/moderator/me ===
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { isModeratorRequest, getModeratorZone } from '@/lib/moderator-session'

export const runtime = 'nodejs'
export const dynamic = 'force-dynamic'

export async function GET(req: NextRequest) {
  const ok = isModeratorRequest(req)
  return NextResponse.json({ moderator: ok, zone: ok ? getModeratorZone(req) : null })
}

--- farmer-session ---
import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'
import type { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
import { getSessionSecret } from '@/lib/session'

// Farmer auth cookie. Separate cookie + namespaced HMAC so a stolen
// consumer or rider token can't be replayed against farmer endpoints.
const COOKIE_NAME = 'yff_farmer'
const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000

function b64url(buf: Buffer): string {
  return buf.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')
}

function sign(payload: string): string {
  const secret=[REDACTED]()
  return b64url(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(`farmer:${payload}`).digest())
}

export function createFarmerSessionToken(farmerId: string): string {
  const issuedAt = Date.now()
  const payload = `${farmerId}.${issuedAt}`
  return `${payload}.${sign(payload)}`
}

export type FarmerSessionPayload = { farmerId: string; issuedAt: number }

export function verifyFarmerSessionToken(token: string | undefined | null): FarmerSessionPayload | null {
  if (!token) return null
  const parts = token.split('.')
  if (parts.length !== 3) return null
  const [farmerId, issuedAtStr, sig] = parts
  const issuedAt = Number(issuedAtStr)
  if (!farmerId || !Number.isFinite(issuedAt)) return null
  if (Date.now() - issuedAt > TOKEN_TTL_MS) return null

  let expected: string
  try {
    expected = sign(`${farmerId}.${issuedAtStr}`)
  } catch {

I have the full picture now. Two findings shape what I build, so let me confirm direction before writing a lot of code:

**1. There's already a `/moderator/farmers/new` page** that implements ~80% of this exact form (name, phone, village, district, method, GPS+address search, photos, pesticide cert, pickup locations, UPI, COD). Your spec adds: bank account/IFSC, soil organic carbon, "registered by this moderator" tracking, and an **activation code** (instead of just sharing a profile link).

**2. The activation code is a brand-new concept.** Farmers currently log in with **phone + password** (`farmers.password_hash`). There's no activation/redemption flow anywhere. The moderators table has IDs, but the moderator session token only carries the zone — I'll need to add the moderator's ID to the session to support "registered by me" / `/moderator/my-farmers`.

These two points create genuine scope decisions:

100	            </div>

111	
112	// Shared client-side auth guard. Returns the zone once confirmed, or redirects
113	// to /moderator/login. Every moderator page uses this so a farmer / consumer /
114	// rider who lands on a /moderator URL is bounced out immediately.
115	export function useModeratorAuth() {
116	  const router = useRouter()
117	  const [zone, setZone] = useState<string | null>(null)
118	  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false)
119	
120	  useEffect(() => {
121	    let cancelled = false
122	    fetch('/api/moderator/me', { credentials: 'same-origin' })
123	      .then((r) => r.json())
124	      .then((json) => {
125	        if (cancelled) return
126	        if (json?.moderator) { setZone(json.zone); setChecked(true) }
127	        else router.replace('/moderator/login')
128	      })
129	      .catch(() => { if (!cancelled) router.replace('/moderator/login') })
130	    return () => { cancelled = true }
131	  }, [router])
132	
133	  return { zone, checked }
134	}
135

25	export default function ModeratorFarmersPage() {

35	    setLoading(false)
36	    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }

51	      body: JSON.stringify({ active: !f.active }),
52	    }).catch(() => null)

84	      )}

  "new_string": "// Token shape: moderator.<issuedAt>.<zoneB64>.<idB64>.<sig>. '', 'utf8'))\n  const payload = `moderator.${issuedAt}.${zoneB64}.${idB64}`\n  return `${payload}.${sign(payload)}`\n}\n\n// Parse + verify the moderator cookie. Caller checks DB uniqueness.\nfunction makeActivationCode(): string {\n  let s = ''\n  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) s += CODE_ALPHABET[randomInt(CODE_ALPHABET.length)]\n  return `YFF-${s}`\n}\n\nfunction svc() {\n  return createClient(\n    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,\n    process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY!,\n  )\n}"
