{
  "file_path": "/home/veerendra/farmer/yourfamilyfarmer/src/app/moderator/register-farmer/page.tsx",
  "content": "'use client'\n\nimport { useState } from 'react'\nimport { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'\nimport { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'\nimport LocationSearch from '@/components/LocationSearch'\nimport ModeratorShell, { useModeratorAuth } from '../ModeratorShell'\n\ntype Created = { id: string; slug: string; name: string; phone: string | null; activation_code: string | null }\n\n// Shrink large camera photos before upload so they stay quick on a 4G connection.\nasync function compressImage(file: File, maxPx = 800, quality = 0.7): Promise<File> {\n  return new Promise((resolve) => {\n    const img = new Image()\n    const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(file)\n    img.onload = () => {\n      URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl)\n      const scale = Math.min(1, maxPx / Math.max(img.naturalWidth, img.naturalHeight))\n      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas')\n      canvas.width = Math.round(img.naturalWidth * scale)\n      canvas.height = Math.round(img.naturalHeight * scale)\n      const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d')\n      if (!ctx) { resolve(file); return }\n      ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height)\n      canvas.toBlob(\n        (blob) => {\n          if (!blob) { resolve(file); return }\n          const name = file.name.replace(/\\.[^.]+$/, '.jpg')\n          resolve(new File([blob], name, { type: 'image/jpeg' }))\n        },\n        'image/jpeg',\n        quality,\n      )\n    }\n    img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(blobUrl); resolve(file) }\n    img.src = blobUrl\n  })\n}\n\ntype PhotoState = { file: File | null; preview: string }\nconst EMPTY_PHOTO: PhotoState = { file: null, preview: '' }\n\nexport default function RegisterFarmerPage() {\n  const router = useRouter()\n  const { zone, checked } = useModeratorAuth()\n\n  const EMPTY_FORM = {\n    name: '', phone: '', village: '', district: '',\n    method: 'natural', farm_size_acres: '', farming_since_year: '', story_quote: '',\n    farm_address: '', upi_id: '', soil_organic_carbon: '',\n    bank_account_number: '', bank_ifsc: '',\n  }\n  const [form, setForm] = useState(EMPTY_FORM)\n  const [error, setError] = useState('')\n  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)\n  const [created, setCreated] = useState<Created | null>(null)\n\n  // Pickup & payout details (same shape as the farmer's own profile editor).\n  const [pickupLocations, setPickupLocations] = useState<string[]>([])\n  const [newPickup, setNewPickup] = useState('')\n  const [codEnabled, setCodEnabled] = useState(false)\n\n  // Farm GPS location (same as the farmer's own profile editor).\n  const [lat, setLat] = useState<number | null>(null)\n  const [lng, setLng] = useState<number | null>(null)\n  const [locationName, setLocationName] = useState('')\n  const [locating, setLocating] = useState(false)\n  const [locError, setLocError] = useState('')\n\n  // Photos: cover, avatar, pesticide cert, UPI QR.\n  const [cover, setCover] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n  const [avatar, setAvatar] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n  const [cert, setCert] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n  const [qr, setQr] = useState<PhotoState>(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n\n  const resetAll = () => {\n    setForm(EMPTY_FORM)\n    setPickupLocations([]); setNewPickup('')\n    setCodEnabled(false)\n    setLat(null); setLng(null); setLocationName(''); setLocError('')\n    for (const p of [cover, avatar, cert, qr]) if (p.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(p.preview)\n    setCover(EMPTY_PHOTO); setAvatar(EMPTY_PHOTO); setCert(EMPTY_PHOTO); setQr(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n  }\n\n  const pickPhoto = (set: (p: PhotoState) => void, current: PhotoState) => async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {\n    const file = e.target.files?.[0]\n    if (!file) return\n    if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) { setError('Please pick an image file.'); return }\n    if (file.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024) { setError('Image is too large (max 8 MB).'); return }\n    setError('')\n    if (current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)\n    const compressed = await compressImage(file)\n    set({ file: compressed, preview: URL.createObjectURL(compressed) })\n  }\n  const clearPhoto = (set: (p: PhotoState) => void, current: PhotoState) => () => {\n    if (current.preview) URL.revokeObjectURL(current.preview)\n    set(EMPTY_PHOTO)\n  }\n\n  const handleGPS = () => {\n    if (!navigator.geolocation) { setLocError('Geolocation not supported on this device.'); return }\n    setLocating(true); setLocError('')\n    navigator.geolocation.getCurrentPosition(\n      (pos) => {\n        setLat(pos.coords.latitude); setLng(pos.coords.longitude)\n        setLocationName(form.village || 'Farm'); setLocating(false)\n      },\n      (err) => {\n        setLocError(err.code === 1\n          ? 'Location permission denied. Allow location in browser settings.'\n          : 'Could not get location. Please try again.')\n        setLocating(false)\n      },\n      { timeout: 15000, enableHighAccuracy: true },\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Upload one photo to the shared farm-images bucket. The farmer row doesn't\n  // exist yet, so namespace under a temp folder generated for this onboarding.\n  const uploadPhoto = async (file: File, folder: string, suffix: string): Promise<string | null> => {\n    const ext = file.name.split('.').pop()?.toLowerCase() || 'jpg'\n    const path = `${folder}/${suffix}-${Date.now()}.${ext}`\n    const { error: upErr } = await supabase.storage\n      .from('farm-images')\n      .upload(path, file, { contentType: file.type, upsert: false })\n    if (upErr) { setError(`Upload failed: ${upErr.message}`); return null }\n    return supabase.storage.from('farm-images').getPublicUrl(path).data.publicUrl\n  }\n\n  const addPickup = () => {\n    const v = newPickup.trim()\n    if (!v || pickupLocations.includes(v)) { setNewPickup(''); return }\n    setPickupLocations((prev) => [...prev, v]); setNewPickup('')\n  }\n  const removePickup = (loc: string) => setPickupLocations((prev) => prev.filter((l) => l !== loc))\n\n  const set = (k: keyof typeof form) => (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement | HTMLTextAreaElement | HTMLSelectElement>) =>\n    setForm((f) => ({ ...f, [k]: e.target.value }))\n\n  const submit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {\n    e.preventDefault()\n    if (submitting) return\n    setError('')\n    setSubmitting(true)\n\n    // Upload any chosen photos first; abort the whole save if an upload fails.\n    const folder = `onboarding-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 8)}`\n    const [coverUrl, avatarUrl, certUrl, qrUrl] = await Promise.all([\n      cover.file ? uploadPhoto(cover.file, folder, 'cover') : Promise.resolve(null),\n      avatar.file ? uploadPhoto(avatar.file, folder, 'avatar') : Promise.resolve(null),\n      cert.file ? uploadPhoto(cert.file, folder, 'pesticide-cert') : Promise.resolve(null),\n      qr.file ? uploadPhoto(qr.file, folder, 'upi-qr') : Promise.resolve(null),\n    ])\n    if ((cover.file && !coverUrl) || (avatar.file && !avatarUrl) || (cert.file && !certUrl) || (qr.file && !qrUrl)) {\n      setSubmitting(false)\n      return // error already set by uploadPhoto\n    }\n\n    const r = await fetch('/api/moderator/farmers', {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      credentials: 'same-origin',\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        ...form,\n        pickup_locations: pickupLocations,\n        cod_enabled: codEnabled,\n        cover_photo_url: coverUrl,\n        photo_url: avatarUrl,\n        pesticide_cert_url: certUrl,\n        upi_qr_code_url: qrUrl,\n        lat, lng,\n        location_name: lat != null && lng != null ? (locationName || form.village) : null,\n      }),\n    }).catch(() => null)\n    setSubmitting(false)\n    if (!r) { setError('Network error.'); return }\n    const json = await r.json().catch(() => ({}))\n    if (!r.ok || !json?.farmer) { setError(json?.error ?? 'Could not save farmer.'); return }\n    setCreated(json.farmer as Created)\n  }\n\n  if (!checked || !zone) {\n    return (\n      <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center\">\n        <div className=\"w-10 h-10 border-4 border-green-700 border-t-transparent rounded-full animate-spin\" />\n      </main>\n    )\n  }\n\n  // Success screen: show the activation code to share + profile link.\n  if (created) {\n    return (\n      <ModeratorShell title=\"Farmer registered\" zone={zone}>\n        <FarmerCreatedCard\n          created={created}\n          onAddAnother={() => { setCreated(null); resetAll() }}\n          onViewMine={() => router.push('/moderator/my-farmers')}\n        />\n      </ModeratorShell>\n    )\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <ModeratorShell title=\"Register new farmer\" subtitle=\"Onboard a farmer on their behalf\" zone={zone}>\n      <form onSubmit={submit} className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-5 max-w-2xl space-y-4\">\n        <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4\">\n          <Field label=\"Full name *\">\n            <input value={form.name} onChange={set('name')} required className={inputCls} autoFocus />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Phone (WhatsApp)\">\n            <input value={form.phone} onChange={set('phone')} placeholder=\"+91 94400 12345\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Village\">\n            <input value={form.village} onChange={set('village')} className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"District\">\n            <input value={form.district} onChange={set('district')} className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Farming method\">\n            <select value={form.method} onChange={set('method')} className={inputCls}>\n              <option value=\"natural\">Natural (no chemicals)</option>\n              <option value=\"organic\">Organic (certified)</option>\n              <option value=\"low_chemical\">Low chemical</option>\n              <option value=\"chemical\">Chemical</option>\n            </select>\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Farm size (acres)\">\n            <input value={form.farm_size_acres} onChange={set('farm_size_acres')} type=\"number\" step=\"0.1\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Farming since (year)\">\n            <input value={form.farming_since_year} onChange={set('farming_since_year')} type=\"number\" placeholder=\"2015\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n          <Field label=\"Soil organic carbon (%)\">\n            <input value={form.soil_organic_carbon} onChange={set('soil_organic_carbon')} type=\"number\" step=\"0.01\" placeholder=\"e.g. 0.85\" className={inputCls} />\n          </Field>\n        </div>\n        <Field label=\"Story / quote\">\n          <textarea value={form.story_quote} onChange={set('story_quote')} rows={3} className={inputCls} />\n        </Field>\n\n        {/* ── Farm location & photos — mirrors the farmer's own profile ── */}\n        <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-4\">\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800 mb-3\">Farm location &amp; photos</p>\n\n          <div>\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farm location (GPS)</span>\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2\">Set the farm location so nearby buyers discover their produce first.</p>\n            {lat != null && lng != null ? (\n              <div className=\"flex items-center justify-between bg-green-50 border border-green-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3\">\n                <span className=\"text-sm font-semibold text-green-800\">✓ 📍 {locationName || 'Location set'}</span>\n                <button type=\"button\" onClick={() => { setLat(null); setLng(null); setLocationName('') }} className=\"text-xs text-green-700 underline font-semibold\">Change</button>\n              </div>\n            ) : (\n              <div className=\"space-y-2\">\n                <button\n                  type=\"button\"\n                  onClick={handleGPS}\n                  disabled={locating}\n                  className=\"w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-700 text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl text-sm active:bg-green-800 disabled:opacity-50\"\n                >\n                  {locating ? 'Getting location…' : '📍 Use GPS (most accurate)'}\n                </button>\n                <div className=\"flex items-center gap-2\">\n                  <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-gray-200\" />\n                  <span className=\"text-[10px] text-gray-400 font-semibold\">OR</span>\n                  <div className=\"flex-1 border-t border-gray-200\" />\n                </div>\n                <LocationSearch\n                  placeholder=\"Search farm location\"\n                  onSelect={(la, ln, nm) => { setLat(la); setLng(ln); setLocationName(nm) }}\n                />\n              </div>\n            )}\n            {locError && <p className=\"text-xs text-red-600 bg-red-50 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 mt-2\">{locError}</p>}\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4\">\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farm cover photo</span>\n              <PhotoUpload preview={cover.preview} onPick={pickPhoto(setCover, cover)} onClear={clearPhoto(setCover, cover)} aspectClass=\"aspect-[3/1]\" />\n            </div>\n            <div>\n              <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Farmer photo</span>\n              <PhotoUpload preview={avatar.preview} onPick={pickPhoto(setAvatar, avatar)} onClear={clearPhoto(setAvatar, avatar)} aspectClass=\"aspect-square max-w-[120px]\" />\n            </div>\n          </div>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4\">\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Pesticide-free / certification photo (optional)</span>\n            <PhotoUpload preview={cert.preview} onPick={pickPhoto(setCert, cert)} onClear={clearPhoto(setCert, cert)} aspectClass=\"aspect-[3/2] max-w-[200px]\" />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {/* ── Pickup & payout — mirrors the farmer's own profile ── */}\n        <div className=\"border-t border-gray-100 pt-4\">\n          <p className=\"text-sm font-extrabold text-green-800 mb-3\">Pickup &amp; payment</p>\n\n          <Field label=\"Farm address (for pickup)\">\n            <textarea value={form.farm_address} onChange={set('farm_address')} rows={2} className={inputCls} placeholder=\"House / street, landmark, village\" />\n          </Field>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4\">\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">Pickup locations</span>\n            <div className=\"flex gap-2\">\n              <input\n                value={newPickup}\n                onChange={(e) => setNewPickup(e.target.value)}\n                onKeyDown={(e) => { if (e.key === 'Enter') { e.preventDefault(); addPickup() } }}\n                placeholder=\"e.g. Market road junction\"\n                className={inputCls}\n              />\n              <button type=\"button\" onClick={addPickup} className=\"bg-green-700 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 rounded-xl active:bg-green-800 whitespace-nowrap\">Add</button>\n            </div>\n            {pickupLocations.length > 0 && (\n              <div className=\"flex flex-wrap gap-2 mt-2\">\n                {pickupLocations.map((loc) => (\n                  <span key={loc}        </div>\n\n          <div className=\"grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-4\">\n            <Field label=\"UPI ID (for payouts)\">\n              <input value={form.upi_id} onChange={set('upi_id')} </div>\n\n          <div className=\"mt-4\">\n            <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">UPI QR code</span>\n            <p className=\"text-[11px] text-gray-500 mb-2\">Buyers scan this to pay. Upload a screenshot of their UPI QR.</p>\n            <PhotoUpload preview={qr.preview} onPick={pickPhoto(setQr, qr)} onClear={clearPhoto(setQr, qr)} aspectClass=\"aspect-square max-w-[160px]\" />\n          </div>\n        </div>\n\n        {error && (\n          <p className=\"text-xs text-red-700 bg-red-50 border border-red-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 font-semibold\">{error}</p>\n        )}\n\n        <div className=\"flex gap-2 pt-1\">\n          <button\n            type=\"submit\"\n            disabled={submitting}\n            className=\"bg-green-800 text-white text-sm font-bold px-5 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-green-900 disabled:opacity-50\"\n          >\n            {submitting ? 'Saving…' : 'Register farmer'}\n          </button>\n          <button\n            type=\"button\"\n            onClick={() => router.push('/moderator/farmers')}\n            className=\"bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-sm font-bold px-5 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-gray-50\"\n          >\n            Cancel\n          </button>\n        </div>\n      </form>\n    </ModeratorShell>\n  )\n}\n\nconst inputCls = 'w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-sm focus:border-green-500 focus:outline-none'\n\nfunction Field({ label, children }: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {\n  return (\n    <label className=\"block\">\n      <span className=\"text-[11px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wide block mb-1\">{label}</span>\n      {children}\n    </label>\n  )\n}\n\n// Success card: shows the shareable activation code (copy + WhatsApp) and the\n// live profile link, then lets the moderator add another or view their list.\nfunction FarmerCreatedCard({\n  created,\n  onAddAnother,\n  onViewMine,\n}: {\n  created: Created\n  onAddAnother: () => void\n  onViewMine: () => void\n}) {\n  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)\n  const code = created.activation_code ?? ''\n  const origin = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window.location.origin : ''\n  const profileUrl = `${origin}/farmer/${created.slug}`\n\n  const copyCode = async () => {\n    try {\n      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(code)\n      setCopied(true)\n      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1800)\n    } catch { /* clipboard blocked — moderator can still read the code */ }\n  }\n\n  const digits = (created.phone ?? '').replace(/\\D/g, '')\n  const waPhone = digits.length === 10 ? `91${digits}` : digits\n  const waText = encodeURIComponent(\n    `Welcome to GoGrameen, ${created.name}! 🌱\\n\\n`\n    + `Your activation code: ${code}\\n`\n    + `Use it to activate your farmer login.\\n\\n`\n    + `Your farm page is live: ${profileUrl}`,\n  )\n  const waLink = `https://wa.me/${waPhone}?text=${waText}`\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 max-w-md\">\n      <div className=\"text-4xl mb-2\">✅</div>\n      <h2 className=\"text-lg font-extrabold text-gray-900\">{created.name} is registered</h2>\n      <p className=\"text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1\">Share this activation code with the farmer:</p>\n\n      {/* Activation code — the headline of this screen. */}\n      <div className=\"mt-3 bg-green-50 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-2xl px-4 py-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-3\">\n        <span className=\"text-2xl md:text-3xl font-black tracking-widest text-green-900 font-mono break-all\">\n          {code || '—'}\n        </span>\n        <button\n          onClick={copyCode}\n          disabled={!code}\n          className=\"bg-green-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded-xl active:bg-green-800 whitespace-nowrap disabled:opacity-50\"\n        >\n          {copied ? 'Copied ✓' : 'Copy'}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <p className=\"text-xs text-gray-500 mt-3\">Their profile page is live:</p>\n      <a href={`/farmer/${created.slug}`} target=\"_blank\" className=\"block text-green-700 underline text-sm mt-1 break-all\">{profileUrl}</a>\n\n      <div className=\"flex flex-col gap-2 mt-5\">\n        {waPhone && (\n          <a\n            href={waLink}\n            target=\"_blank\"\n            className=\"bg-green-600 text-white text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl text-center active:bg-green-700\"\n          >\n            Send code via WhatsApp\n          </a>\n        )}\n        <button\n          onClick={onAddAnother}\n          className=\"bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-700 text-sm font-bold px-4 py-3 rounded-xl active:bg-gray-50\"\n        >\n          + Register another farmer\n        </button>\n        <button onClick={onViewMine} className=\"text-gray-500 text-sm underline\">\n          View farmers I registered\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  )\n}\n\nfunction PhotoUpload({\n  preview,\n  onPick,\n  onClear,\n  aspectClass,\n}: {\n  preview: string\n  onPick: (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => void\n  onClear: () => void\n  aspectClass: string\n}) {\n  if (preview) {\n    return (\n      <div className={`relative ${aspectClass} rounded-xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 bg-gray-50`}>\n        {/* eslint-disable-next-line @next/next/no-img-element */}\n        <img src={preview} alt=\"\" className=\"w-full h-full object-cover\" />\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          onClick={onClear}\n          className=\"absolute top-2 right-2 bg-white/90 text-gray-700 rounded-full w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center text-sm font-bold shadow\"\n        >\n          ×\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    )\n  }\n  return (\n    <label className=\"flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-2 border-dashed border-green-300 rounded-xl py-4 px-2 text-green-700 text-xs font-bold cursor-pointer active:bg-green-50\">\n      <span>📷</span> Add photo\n      <input type=\"file\" accept=\"image/*\" onChange={onPick} className=\"hidden\" />\n    </label>\n  )\n}\n"
}

> AGENT

Both new routes built, the old one redirects, and the build is green. Here's what shipped.

## New moderator farmer-registration feature

**Pages**
- **`/moderator/register-farmer`** — the full onboarding form. All the fields the farmer self-registration captures (name, phone, village, district, GPS + address-search location, method, pickup locations, UPI, COD, cover/farmer/pesticide-cert photos, UPI QR) **plus your new fields**: soil organic carbon (%), bank account number, IFSC. On submit it shows a success screen with the **activation code in big mono type, a Copy button, and a "Send code via WhatsApp"** link that pre-fills the code + live profile URL.
- **`/moderator/my-farmers`** — lists only the farmers *this* moderator registered, with activation code (copy button), method, listing count, and Active/Inactive status.
- **`/moderator/farmers/new`** → now a permanent redirect to `/moderator/register-farmer` (single canonical form, no duplication). The "Add new farmer" button and a new **"My farmers"** sidebar item point at the new routes.

**Backend**
- **Migration** `farmers_moderator_registration` (applied to Supabase + saved to `scripts/`): adds `activation_code`, `registered_by_moderator` (FK → `moderators.id`), `bank_account_number`, `bank_ifsc`. `soil_organic_carbon` already existed and is now populated.
- **Moderator session now carries the moderator's ID** (was zone-only). Backward-compatible: old 4-part tokens still validate (id = null); the `my-farmers` page shows a "please re-login" banner for those rather than leaking the whole zone.
- **`POST /api/moderator/farmers`** generates a unique `YFF-XXXX` code (unambiguous alphabet, no 0/O/1/I/L), stamps `registered_by_moderator` from the session, validates IFSC, and returns the code. **`GET ...?mine=1`** filters to the moderator's own farmers.

Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean and `npm run build` succeeds (both new routes + the redirect compiled).

## Two things to flag (per your choices)
- **Activation code is "generate + display only"** — farmers still log in with phone + password. The redemption page (farmer enters phone + `YFF-XXXX` → sets password) is **not built yet**; that was the agreed follow-up. So the code is shareable/stored now but isn't yet wired into farmer login.
- **Bank account/IFSC are stored as plaintext columns** (as chosen), like the existing `upi_id` — readable by anyone with service-role/DB access.

This is all **uncommitted**. Want me to commit it (e.g. `feat(moderator): farmer registration form with activation code + my-farmers list`)? I won't push without your OK.

Also — earlier this session I left the courier-timeline/notifications work uncommitted on top of `93f77c3`. Want both folded into one commit, or kept as two separate commits?

> AGENT

Good news — **this is already fully built and pushed.** Everything you described is in the three commits we just sent up (`93f77c3` → `d8b22fc`). Here's each of your points mapped to the live code:

| Your requirement | Where it's implemented | How it behaves |
|---|---|---|
| After farmer sets date, farmer can say **"Picked Up"** or **"Shipped"** | `farmer/dashboard/page.tsx:3170` & `:3197` | An approved order shows **"✓ Picked Up / తీసుకువెళ్ళారు"** (self-pickup orders) or **"📦 Shipped / షిప్ చేయబడింది"** (courier orders) — which one depends on the delivery method the buyer chose at checkout |
| **Picked Up** → consumer collects → ends active order → **History** | `isResolved()` at `:120-123` (`collected_at` set) | Tapping it stamps `collected_at`; the order immediately drops out of Active Orders into Order History |
| **Shipped** → farmer shipped it | `handleMarkShipped` → `shipped_at` | Stamps `shipped_at` but the order **stays active**, showing "Awaiting buyer's receipt confirmation" |
| Consumer says **"Received"** → ends active order → **History** | `consumer/orders/[id]/page.tsx:358-364` | The **"✓ Received / అందుకున్నాను"** button appears once shipped; tapping it stamps `received_at`, which resolves the order and moves it to history on both sides |

So the exact lifecycle you wrote is in place:
- **Pickup:** Placed → Payment → Confirmed (date set) → **Picked Up** → History
- **Courier:** Placed → Payment → Confirmed (date set) → **Shipped** → **Received** → History

Plus: both sides get a notification on status change, and the buyer's order page shows the full dated timeline.

**The most likely reason you're not seeing it:** the branch is pushed but probably **not deployed to Vercel/production yet** — so you're still looking at the old build. The code is correct on the `feat/suspend-consumer-reason-privacy` branch.

```
1	import { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from 'crypto'
2	import type { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server'
3	
4	// HMAC-signed session token (server-only). Stored in an HTTP-only cookie so
5	// XSS cannot exfiltrate it. Format: <consumerId>.<issuedAtMs>.<hmacHexBase64url>
6	//
7	// We never put consumer name or phone in the token — those live in the DB.
8	// localStorage holds the public profile copy used purely for UI display, and
9	// the server re-derives identity from the cookie on every request.
10	
11	const COOKIE_NAME = 'yff_consumer'
12	const TOKEN_TTL_MS = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 // 30 days
13	
14	export function getSessionSecret(): string {
15	  const secret = process.env.SESSION_SECRET
16	  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
17	    throw new Error(
18	      'SESSION_SECRET env var is missing or too short. Set a 32+ char random string in your environment (e.g. `openssl rand -hex 32`).',
19	    )
20	  }
21	  return secret
22	}
23	
24	function b64url(buf: Buffer): string {
25	  return buf.toString('base64').replace(/\+/g, '-').replace(/\//g, '_').replace(/=+$/, '')
26	}
27	
28	function sign(payload: string): string {
29	  const secret=[REDACTED]()
30	  return b64url(createHmac('sha256', secret).update(payload).digest())
31	}
32	
33	export function createSessionToken(consumerId: string): string {
34	  const issuedAt = Date.now()
35	  const payload = `${consumerId}.${issuedAt}`
36	  const sig = sign(payload)
37	  return `${payload}.${sig}`
38	}
39	
40	export type SessionPayload = { consumerId: string; issuedAt: number }
41	
42	export function verifySessionToken(token: string | undefined | null): SessionPayload | null {
43	  if (!token) return null
44	  const parts = token.split('.')
45	  if (parts.length !== 3) return null
46	  const [consumerId, issuedAtStr, sig] = parts
47	  const issuedAt = Number(issuedAtStr)
48	  if (!consumerId || !Number.isFinite(issuedAt)) return null
49	  if (Date.now() - issuedAt > TOKEN_TTL_MS) return null
50	
51	  let expected: string
52	  try {
53	    expected = sign(`${consumerId}.${issuedAtStr}`)
54	  } catch {
55	    return null
56	  }
57	  const a = Buffer.from(sig)
58	  const b = Buffer.from(expected)
59	  if (a.length !== b.length) return null
60	  if (!timingSafeEqual(a, b)) return null
61	  return { consumerId, issuedAt }
62	}
63	
64	export function getConsumerSessionFromRequest(req: NextRequest): SessionPayload | null {
65	  const token = req.cookies.get(COOKIE_NAME)?.value
```

> AGENT

Clean shutdown, all test data removed, no stray processes. Here's the verdict.

## Verification: order-completion flow (Picked Up / Shipped / Received → History)

**Verdict: PASS** ✅

**Claim:** After a farmer confirms an order, they mark self-pickup orders **Picked Up** (resolves → history) or courier orders **Shipped** (stays active); the buyer then taps **Received** on a shipped courier order (resolves → history). Guards prevent invalid transitions.

**Method:** Ran the real app (`next dev`, port 3000) against the Supabase backend. Created throwaway test data (one farmer, one consumer, three approved orders), minted valid HMAC session cookies the same way the app signs them, drove the actual HTTP API the buttons call, then deleted all test data.

### Steps (against the running server)

1. ✅ Farmer → `POST /api/farmer/orders/{pickup}/picked-up` → `HTTP 200 {"ok":true,"collected_at":"…"}`
2. ✅ Farmer → `POST /api/farmer/orders/{courier}/ship` → `HTTP 200 {"ok":true,"shipped_at":"…"}`
3. ✅ Consumer → `POST /api/consumer/orders/{courier}/received` → `HTTP 200 {"ok":true,"received_at":"…"}`
4. 🔍 P1 Received again (already received) → `409 "already marked received"`
5. 🔍 P2 Received on a self-pickup order → `409 "Only a courier order is confirmed this way"`
6. 🔍 P3 Ship a self-pickup order → `409 "Only a courier order can be marked shipped"`
7. 🔍 P4 Picked-up again (already collected) → `409 "already marked picked up"`
8. 🔍 P5 Picked-up a courier order → `409 "Only a self-pickup order can be marked picked up here"`
9. 🔍 P6 Ship with no cookie → `401 "Please log in."`
10. 🔍 P7 Received on an **un-shipped** courier order → `409 "This order has not been shipped yet."`

**Final DB state** (drives the active→history move via `isResolved()`):

| Order | type | collected | shipped | received | Resolved → History? |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Tomatoes | self_pickup | ✅ | — | — | **Yes** (`collected_at` set) |
| Mangoes | courier | — | ✅ | ✅ | **Yes** (`received_at` set) |
| Bananas | courier | — | — | — | No — stays active (correct) |

`isResolved()` (dashboard `:120-123`) keys off exactly these timestamps: self-pickup resolves on `collected_at`, courier on `received_at`, and a shipped-but-unreceived courier stays active. So the resolved orders drop out of the farmer's active list and the buyer's `orderDone` gate — i.e. move to history — precisely as specified.

### Findings
- 🔍 Every invalid transition (7 probes) returns a clear, correctly-coded `409`/`401` with a bilingual-friendly message — strong server-side guarding, not just UI gating. So even though the buttons hide the wrong action, the API independently enforces it.
- ⚠️ Note for context: this exercised the API surface the buttons POST to (with real auth), not pixels — I didn't drive the rendered buttons under a browser. The button wiring itself is the thin client mapping I confirmed by reading (`dashboard:3170/3197`, `consumer page:358`); the behavioral logic all lives in these routes, which passed end-to-end.
- The feature works on the **branch** — remember production won't show it until this branch is deployed.

So the flow you described is implemented correctly and verified live. Want me to open a PR / help deploy it now?

=== the 4b0634b commit: what it changed re: hiding reason ===
commit 4b0634b801abf1fe6d11e1b69ba3dca9dfcecc69
Author: veerendra <singampalliveerendra5@gmail.com>
Date:   Tue Jun 16 18:33:39 2026 +0530

    feat(moderator): suspend consumer with mandatory reason; hide reason from buyer
    
    Moderators must give a reason to suspend a consumer; the reason is saved
    and shown to moderators in their consumers view. The consumer themselves

> AGENT

I dug into the current code, and this request is important because it **reverses a decision already shipped on this branch.** Here's exactly what I understand.

## What the client is asking for
When a moderator suspends a consumer, the moderator types a **reason**. The client wants that reason **displayed to the consumer themselves** — as a highlighted banner in the consumer app's header — so the suspended user can see *why* they were suspended.

## Why this matters: it contradicts what's already built
Your branch's first commit (`4b0634b`, *"suspend consumer with mandatory reason; hide reason from buyer"*) deliberately did the **opposite**:

- Moderator suspends with a mandatory reason → stored in `consumers_auth.suspended_reason` ✅
- That reason is shown **only to moderators**.
- The consumer is intentionally shown a **generic** message — *"Your account has been suspended. Please contact support."* — at login **and** in the header banner. The real reason is hidden from them on purpose.

So someone previously decided buyers should *not* see the reason. The client is now asking to **undo that** and show the real reason to the consumer.

## The good news: the header banner already exists
The "Consumer header highlighting the suspension" the client describes is **already there** — `GlobalNav.tsx` renders a red suspension banner in the header (`suspendedReason`). It's just being fed the generic text. The only reason the real reason doesn't show is that two endpoints strip it out:
- `consumer/me` returns `suspendedReason: null` (deliberately)
- `consumer/login` returns the generic string

So implementing this is **small** — surface the real `suspended_reason` through those two spots, and the existing header banner will display it. No new UI or schema needed.

## What I need you to confirm
Because this reverses a shipped, intentional choice, I want your explicit go-ahead before changing it:

Typecheck and build both clean. Here's the summary.

## What I understood (and confirmed with you)

Your client's request **reverses** the behavior shipped in commit `4b0634b`. That commit deliberately **hid** the moderator's suspension reason from the consumer (they saw only a generic *"contact support"* message). The client now wants the **real reason shown to the consumer** — highlighted in the app header. You confirmed: yes, show it, in **both the header banner and the login screen**.

## What I changed
The header banner, `ConsumerAuthContext`, and the generic fallback already existed — the reason was only being stripped out at two API endpoints. So this was a small, targeted change:

1. **`api/consumer/me`** — now returns the real `suspended_reason` (was hard-coded to `null`). The existing red header banner in `GlobalNav` automatically picks it up and displays it.
2. **`api/consumer/login`** — a suspended sign-in attempt now shows *"Your account has been suspended. Reason: \<reason\>. Please contact support."* (bilingual EN/Telugu) instead of the generic line.

Both keep a **generic fallback** for any legacy suspension that has no recorded reason. No schema or UI changes were needed.

Verified: `tsc --noEmit` clean, `npm run build` succeeds.

This is **uncommitted**. Two notes before you decide next steps:
- Since this undoes part of `4b0634b`, the branch name (`feat/suspend-consumer-reason-privacy`) is now a bit of a misnomer — the privacy behavior is being reversed per the client.
